=== ROOT CONFIG: README.md ===
# 4-player Pong over UDP (Team 11)

Standard library only. Run everything from this folder (`pong/`).

## Run the tests
    python3 -m unittest discover -v

## Play on one machine
    python3 -m game.server                       # terminal 1
    python3 -m game.client --bot                 # terminal 2: bot (shows the TUI)
    python3 -m game.client --bot                 # terminal 3: another bot
    python3 -m game.client                       # terminal 4: YOU (keyboard)

Controls: left/right-wall players use W/S (or Up/Down); top/bottom-wall players use
A/D (or Left/Right). Space stops, Q quits. `--sticky` makes a key press persist.
Any mix of humans and bots works; a game starts as soon as 2 players are in.

## Experiment mode (no UI)
    python3 -m game.server --log results/raw/server.csv
    python3 -m game.client --server <ip> --bot --headless --duration 60 --log results/raw/h1.csv

`GAME_DROP_PROB=0.1` (or `--drop 0.1`) makes a process discard 10% of received packets,
for testing loss handling without Mininet.

## Mininet (run in the Ubuntu VM, as root)
    sudo apt install -y ethtool                              # once
    sudo python3 -B topo/topology.py --smoke 15              # automated check of the whole setup
    sudo python3 -B topo/topology.py --cli --bots 3          # 3 bots + server; you join from another tab
    python3 -m analysis.metrics results/raw/smoke/*.csv      # latency / jitter / loss table

Topology: h1-h4 (players) and hb1 (background source) on s1; hs (server) and hb2 (background
sink) on s2; s1<->s2 is the 10 Mbit/s bottleneck, shaped in both directions by an OVS QoS.
`--qos plain` is the baseline FIFO, `--qos priority` adds queue 1 (game) and 2 (background),
`--controller remote` waits for a Ryu controller on 127.0.0.1:6653.

## SDN controllers (Ryu, Python 3.9 environment in ~/ryu-env)
    # terminal 1: the controller
    cd ~/CN_project/pong
    PYTHONPATH=. ~/ryu-env/bin/ryu-manager sdn/baseline_switch.py     # or sdn/qos_switch.py
    # terminal 2: the experiment (same controller must be running)
    sudo python3 -B topo/topology.py --smoke 15 --bg-mbps 20 --controller remote --qos plain      # baseline
    sudo python3 -B topo/topology.py --smoke 15 --bg-mbps 20 --controller remote --qos priority   # with qos_switch.py

`baseline_switch.py` is a plain L2 learning switch; `qos_switch.py` subclasses it and only adds
"UDP port 5000 -> queue 1, everything else -> queue 2".  Do not run `sudo mn -c` while Ryu is
running: it kills ryu-manager.

## Layout
    game/config.py    constants shared with Mininet + Ryu (UDP port 5000, tick rate, ...)
    game/protocol.py  wire format
    game/seq.py       sequence tracker (loss / duplicate / late detection)
    game/state.py     game simulation (pure logic)
    game/server.py    UDP server (receive thread + tick thread)
    game/client.py    UDP client (human or bot)
    game/bot.py       computer player
    game/tui.py       curses UI + keyboard input
    game/signals.py   SIGTERM == Ctrl-C so experiment scripts can stop processes cleanly
    sdn/baseline_switch.py  baseline controller (learning switch)
    sdn/qos_switch.py       priority controller (game -> queue 1)
    topo/topology.py  Mininet network, bottleneck QoS, smoke test, demo mode
    analysis/metrics.py  latency / jitter / loss from the CSV logs


=== FOLDER: /analysis ===
=== FILE: metrics.py ===
"""Latency / jitter / loss from the CSV logs written by game.client and game.server.

Standard library only, so it also runs inside the Mininet VM.

    python3 -m analysis.metrics results/raw/smoke/*.csv

Definitions (per packet stream):
  delay   one-way delay in ms = recv_time - send_time.  Valid because every Mininet
          host shares the same kernel clock (time.monotonic).
  jitter  mean absolute difference between the delays of consecutive arrivals (ms).
  loss    1 - (unique packets received) / (highest seq - lowest seq + 1).
          Late (out-of-order) packets count as received; duplicates are ignored.
  rtt     PING -> PONG round trip in ms (client logs only).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import sys
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

Row = Tuple[str, float, int, float]      # kind, recv_time, seq, send_time
ARRIVED = ("new", "late")                # kinds that count as "the packet arrived"


def _percentile(sorted_vals: List[float], p: float) -> float:
    if not sorted_vals:
        return 0.0
    k = (len(sorted_vals) - 1) * p / 100.0
    lo = int(k)
    hi = min(lo + 1, len(sorted_vals) - 1)
    return sorted_vals[lo] + (sorted_vals[hi] - sorted_vals[lo]) * (k - lo)


def summarize(rows: Iterable[Row], warmup: float = 0.0, tail: float = 0.0) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """Summarise ONE packet stream.  `rows` must be in arrival order.
    
    Excludes the first `warmup` seconds and last `tail` seconds based on recv_time.
    """
    rows = list(rows)
    if not rows:
        return {"n": 0, "expected": 0, "lost": 0, "loss_pct": 0.0,
                "delay_mean_ms": 0.0, "delay_p50_ms": 0.0, "delay_p95_ms": 0.0,
                "delay_max_ms": 0.0, "jitter_ms": 0.0}
    
    recv_times = [r[1] for r in rows]
    t_min, t_max = min(recv_times), max(recv_times)
    t_start = t_min + warmup
    t_end = t_max - tail
    
    seqs = set()
    delays: List[float] = []
    for kind, recv, seq, send in rows:
        if warmup > 0 and recv < t_start:
            continue
        if tail > 0 and recv > t_end:
            continue
        if kind in ARRIVED:
            seqs.add(seq)
            delays.append((recv - send) * 1000.0)
    n = len(seqs)
    expected = (max(seqs) - min(seqs) + 1) if seqs else 0
    lost = expected - n
    jitter = (sum(abs(b - a) for a, b in zip(delays, delays[1:])) / (len(delays) - 1)) if len(delays) > 1 else 0.0
    ordered = sorted(delays)
    return {
        "n": n,
        "expected": expected,
        "lost": lost,
        "loss_pct": 100.0 * lost / expected if expected else 0.0,
        "delay_mean_ms": sum(delays) / len(delays) if delays else 0.0,
        "delay_p50_ms": _percentile(ordered, 50),
        "delay_p95_ms": _percentile(ordered, 95),
        "delay_max_ms": ordered[-1] if ordered else 0.0,
        "jitter_ms": jitter,
    }


def read_client_csv(path: str) -> Tuple[List[Row], List[float]]:
    """-> (broadcast rows, rtt samples in ms)"""
    rows: List[Row] = []
    rtts: List[float] = []
    with open(path, newline="") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            recv, seq, send = float(r["recv_time"]), int(r["seq"]), float(r["send_time"])
            if r["kind"] == "pong":
                rtts.append((recv - send) * 1000.0)
            else:
                rows.append((r["kind"], recv, seq, send))
    return rows, rtts


def read_server_csv(path: str) -> Dict[int, List[Row]]:
    """-> {player slot: STATE_UPDATE rows}"""
    out: Dict[int, List[Row]] = {}
    with open(path, newline="") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            out.setdefault(int(r["player"]), []).append(
                (r["kind"], float(r["recv_time"]), int(r["seq"]), float(r["send_time"])))
    return out


def summarize_client(path: str, warmup: float = 0.0, tail: float = 0.0) -> Dict[str, float]:
    rows, rtts = read_client_csv(path)
    s = summarize(rows, warmup=warmup, tail=tail)
    ordered = sorted(rtts)
    s["rtt_n"] = len(rtts)
    s["rtt_mean_ms"] = sum(rtts) / len(rtts) if rtts else 0.0
    s["rtt_p95_ms"] = _percentile(ordered, 95)
    return s


def summarize_server(path: str, warmup: float = 0.0, tail: float = 0.0) -> Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]:
    return {slot: summarize(rows, warmup=warmup, tail=tail) for slot, rows in sorted(read_server_csv(path).items())}


def format_table(entries: List[Tuple[str, Dict[str, float]]]) -> str:
    head = "%-22s %7s %7s %9s %9s %9s %10s %9s" % (
        "stream", "pkts", "loss%", "mean ms", "p95 ms", "max ms", "jitter ms", "rtt ms")
    lines = [head, "-" * len(head)]
    for name, s in entries:
        rtt = "%9.2f" % s["rtt_mean_ms"] if s.get("rtt_n") else "%9s" % "-"
        lines.append("%-22s %7d %7.2f %9.3f %9.3f %9.3f %10.3f %s" % (
            name, s["n"], s["loss_pct"], s["delay_mean_ms"], s["delay_p95_ms"],
            s["delay_max_ms"], s["jitter_ms"], rtt))
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    paths = (argv if argv is not None else sys.argv[1:])
    if not paths:
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    entries: List[Tuple[str, Dict[str, float]]] = []
    for path in paths:
        with open(path, newline="") as f:
            first = f.readline().split(",")[0].strip()
        name = path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        if first == "kind":                      # client log
            entries.append((name, summarize_client(path)))
        else:                                    # server log: one stream per player
            for slot, s in summarize_server(path).items():
                entries.append(("%s [P%d up]" % (name, slot + 1), s))
    print(format_table(entries))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

=== FOLDER: /analysis ===
=== FILE: graphs.py ===
"""Graph generation for the experiment results.

Reads results/aggregate.csv and writes PNG files:
    results/graphs/delay_by_bg.png        mean & p95 by background level (baseline vs priority)
    results/graphs/jitter_by_bg.png       jitter by background level
    results/graphs/loss_by_bg.png         loss % by background level
    results/graphs/delay_vs_players.png  delay vs player count under heavy load

Uses matplotlib (available in the VM environment).  Standard library + matplotlib.

    python3 -m analysis.graphs [results/aggregate.csv] [--out-dir results/graphs]
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import os
import sys

# matplotlib check
try:
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
except ImportError as exc:
    raise SystemExit("matplotlib required for graphs (install in VM: pip install matplotlib): %s" % exc)


COLORS = {"baseline": "#1f77b4", "priority": "#ff7f0e"}


def load_aggregate(path: str):
    rows = []
    with open(path, newline="") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            rows.append({
                "mode": r["mode"],
                "bg": r["bg_label"],
                "players": int(r["players"]),
                "bg_mbps": float(r["bg_mbps"]),
                "metric": r["metric"],
                "mean": float(r["mean"]),
                "std": float(r["std"]),
                "n": int(r["n"]),
            })
    return rows


def plot_delay_by_bg(data, out_path):
    # Filter delay_mean_ms and delay_p95_ms; group by bg and mode
    bg_order = ["none", "moderate", "heavy"]
    modes = ["baseline", "priority"]
    x = range(len(bg_order))
    width = 0.35
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5))
    for i, mode in enumerate(modes):
        means = []
        p95s = []
        for bg in bg_order:
            d = [r for r in data if r["mode"] == mode and r["bg"] == bg and r["metric"] == "delay_mean_ms"]
            d95 = [r for r in data if r["mode"] == mode and r["bg"] == bg and r["metric"] == "delay_p95_ms"]
            means.append(d[0]["mean"] if d else 0)
            p95s.append(d95[0]["mean"] if d95 else 0)
        offset = -width / 2 + i * width
        ax.bar([xx + offset for xx in x], means, width, label=f"{mode} mean", color=COLORS[mode], alpha=0.8, edgecolor="black", zorder=3)
        ax.scatter([xx + offset for xx in x], p95s, label=f"{mode} p95", color=COLORS[mode], marker="o", zorder=4, s=50)
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(bg_order)
    ax.set_ylabel("Delay (ms)")
    ax.set_title("Delay by background level (mean + p95)")
    ax.legend(loc="upper right")
    ax.set_yscale("log")
    ax.grid(axis="y", linestyle="--", alpha=0.4)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_jitter_by_bg(data, out_path):
    bg_order = ["none", "moderate", "heavy"]
    modes = ["baseline", "priority"]
    x = range(len(bg_order))
    width = 0.35
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5))
    for i, mode in enumerate(modes):
        vals = []
        for bg in bg_order:
            d = [r for r in data if r["mode"] == mode and r["bg"] == bg and r["metric"] == "jitter_ms"]
            vals.append(d[0]["mean"] if d else 0)
        offset = -width / 2 + i * width
        ax.bar([xx + offset for xx in x], vals, width, label=mode, color=COLORS[mode], alpha=0.8, edgecolor="black")
    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(bg_order)
    ax.set_ylabel("Jitter (ms)")
    ax.set_title("Jitter by background level")
    ax.legend()
    ax.grid(axis="y", linestyle="--", alpha=0.4)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_loss_by_bg(data, out_path):
    bg_order = ["none", "moderate", "heavy"]

    ax.set_xticks(x)

    ax.set_title("Server-side packet loss by background level")
    ax.legend()
    ax.grid(axis="y", linestyle="--", alpha=0.4)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_delay_vs_players(data, out_path):
    # Heavy load only, delay_mean_ms
    heavy = [r for r in data if r["bg"] == "heavy" and r["metric"] == "delay_mean_ms"]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7, 5))
    for mode in ["baseline", "priority"]:
        points = [(r["players"], r["mean"]) for r in heavy if r["mode"] == mode]
        points.sort()
        if points:
            xs, ys = zip(*points)
            ax.plot(xs, ys, marker="o", label=mode, color=COLORS[mode])
    ax.set_xlabel("Players")
    ax.set_ylabel("Mean delay (ms)")
    ax.set_title("Delay vs player count under heavy load")
    ax.set_yscale("log")
    ax.legend()
    ax.grid(linestyle="--", alpha=0.4)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    import argparse
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("aggregate", default="results/aggregate.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--out-dir", default="results/graphs")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if not os.path.exists(args.aggregate):
        print("aggregate.csv not found at %s" % args.aggregate)
        return 1

    data = load_aggregate(args.aggregate)
    os.makedirs(args.out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    plot_delay_by_bg(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "delay_by_bg.png"))
    plot_jitter_by_bg(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "jitter_by_bg.png"))
    plot_loss_by_bg(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "loss_by_bg.png"))
    plot_server_delay_by_bg(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "server_delay_by_bg.png"))
    plot_server_p95_by_bg(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "server_p95_by_bg.png"))
    plot_server_jitter_by_bg(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "server_jitter_by_bg.png"))
    plot_server_loss_by_bg(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "server_loss_by_bg.png"))
    plot_delay_vs_players(data, os.path.join(args.out_dir, "delay_vs_players.png"))

    print("Graphs saved to %s/" % args.out_dir)
    for f in os.listdir(args.out_dir):
        if f.endswith(".png"):
            p = os.path.join(args.out_dir, f)
            sz = os.path.getsize(p)
            print("  %s (%d bytes)" % (f, sz))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

=== FOLDER: /analysis ===
=== FILE: report.py ===
"""Aggregation: mean ± std over repeats, baseline-vs-priority improvement.

Reads results/summary.csv (written by topo/run_scenario.py) and writes:
    results/aggregate.csv         (summary table)
    results/analysis.md           (markdown tables with improvement factors)

Standard library only.

    python3 -m analysis.report [results/summary.csv] [--out results/aggregate.csv] [--md results/analysis.md]
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import math
import os
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple


def mean_std(values: List[float]) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    if not values:
        return 0.0, 0.0
    m = sum(values) / len(values)
    variance = sum((v - m) ** 2 for v in values) / len(values)
    return m, math.sqrt(variance)


def group_by_key(rows: List[dict]) -> Dict[Tuple[str, str, int, float], List[dict]]:
    out: Dict[Tuple[str, str, int, float], List[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in rows:
        key = (r["mode"], r["bg_label"], int(r["players"]), float(r["bg_mbps"]))
        out[key].append(r)
    return out


def aggregate(summary_path: str) -> List[dict]:
    with open(summary_path, newline="") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        rows = list(reader)
    groups = group_by_key(rows)
    out = []
    for key in sorted(groups):
        mode, bg, players, bg_mbps = key
        grp = groups[key]
        for metric in ("delay_mean_ms", "delay_p95_ms", "jitter_ms", "loss_pct",
                       "rtt_mean_ms", "rtt_p95_ms",
                       "server_delay_mean_ms", "server_delay_p95_ms",
                       "server_jitter_ms", "server_loss_pct"):
            vals = [float(r[metric]) for r in grp if r.get(metric) not in ("", None)]
            m, s = mean_std(vals)
            out.append({
                "mode": mode, "bg_label": bg, "players": players, "bg_mbps": bg_mbps,
                "metric": metric, "mean": m, "std": s, "n": len(vals),
            })
    return out


def improvement_factor(baseline: float, priority: float) -> float:
    if baseline <= 0 or priority <= 0:
        return 0.0
    # For delay/jitter/loss: lower is better => improvement = baseline / priority
    # (e.g., baseline 10ms, priority 2ms => 5x lower)
    return baseline / priority


def format_table(entries: List[dict]) -> str:
    head = "%-12s %-12s %3s %4s %10s %10s %4s" % (
        "mode", "bg", "pl", "mgps", "mean", "std", "n")
    lines = [head, "-" * len(head)]
    for e in entries:
        lines.append("%-12s %-12s %3d %4.1f %10.3f %10.3f %4d" % (
            e["mode"], e["bg_label"], e["players"], e["bg_mbps"],
            e["mean"], e["std"], e["n"]))
    return "\n".join(lines)


def generate_markdown(aggregate_rows: List[dict], md_path: str, summary_path: str) -> None:
    agg = aggregate_rows

    # Build comparison table: baseline vs priority per (bg, players, metric)
    # We compute improvement factors from paired group means
    lines = ["# Experiment Results", ""]
    lines.append("Source: `%s`" % summary_path)
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("## Aggregate (mean ± std over repeats)")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("| mode | bg | players | metric | mean | std | n |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for r in agg:
        lines.append("| %s | %s | %s | %s | %.3f | %.3f | %s |" % (
            r["mode"], r["bg_label"], r["players"], r["metric"],
            float(r["mean"]), float(r["std"]), r["n"]))
    lines.append("")

    # Add improvement table (only for delay_mean_ms)
    lines.append("## Baseline vs Priority — delay improvement")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("| bg | players | baseline mean | priority mean | factor |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|")
    # Pair up by (bg, players, metric=delay_mean_ms)
    baseline = {}
    priority = {}
    for r in agg:
        if r["metric"] == "delay_mean_ms":
            key = (r["bg_label"], int(r["players"]))
            if r["mode"] == "baseline":
                baseline[key] = float(r["mean"])
            else:
                priority[key] = float(r["mean"])
    for key in sorted(baseline):
        if key in priority:
            b = baseline[key]
            p = priority[key]
            factor = improvement_factor(b, p)
            lines.append("| %s | %d | %.3f | %.3f | %.2fx |" % (key[0], key[1], b, p, factor))
    lines.append("")

    with open(md_path, "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(lines))


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("summary", default="results/summary.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="results/aggregate.csv")
    ap.add_argument("--md", default="results/analysis.md")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    rows = aggregate(args.summary)
    with open(args.out, "w", newline="") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(["mode", "bg_label", "players", "bg_mbps", "metric",
                    "mean", "std", "n"])
        for r in rows:
            w.writerow([r["mode"], r["bg_label"], r["players"], r["bg_mbps"],
                        r["metric"], f"{r['mean']:.4f}", f"{r['std']:.4f}", r["n"]])

    generate_markdown(rows, args.md, args.summary)
    print("aggregate.csv -> %s" % args.out)
    print("analysis.md -> %s" % args.md)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

=== FOLDER: /topo ===
=== FILE: scenarios.py ===
"""Scenario matrix for the baseline-vs-priority experiment.

One place defines the matrix: every combination of
    controller mode (baseline / priority)
    background traffic (none 0 / moderate 9 / heavy 20 Mbit/s UDP each way)
    player count (2 / 3 / 4)
    repeat (1..N)
plus the run-folder naming convention used by run_scenario.py.

    python3 -m topo.scenarios --list
    python3 -m topo.scenarios --quick

Standard library only, so it also runs inside the Mininet VM.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from game import config as C

# --- the matrix ----------------------------------------------------------------

CONTROLLERS = ("baseline", "priority")
# background traffic: (label, Mbit/s UDP each way on the 10 Mbit/s link)
BACKGROUND = (("none", 0.0), ("moderate", 9.0), ("heavy", 20.0))
PLAYERS = (2, 3, 4)
REPEATS = 3

# seconds of game per scenario; the first WARMUP and the last TAIL are excluded
# from the analysis (analysis/metrics.py warmup/tail).
WARMUP = 2.0          # warm-up before background traffic starts
TAIL = 1.0            # tail after the last background packet
DURATION = 15         # total run per scenario


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Scenario:
    """One row of the matrix."""

    mode: str            # "baseline" or "priority"
    bg_label: str
    bg_mbps: float
    players: int
    repeat: int

    @property
    def qos(self) -> str:
        return "plain" if self.mode == "baseline" else "priority"

    @property
    def controller(self) -> str:
        return "none" if self.mode == "baseline" else "remote"

    @property
    def controller_file(self) -> str:
        return ("sdn/baseline_switch.py" if self.mode == "baseline"
                else "sdn/qos_switch.py")

    @property
    def folder_name(self) -> str:
        return "%s_%s_%dp_r%d" % (self.mode, self.bg_label, self.players, self.repeat)

    @property
    def human_name(self) -> str:
        return "%s / %s bg / %d players / repeat %d" % (
            self.mode, self.bg_label, self.players, self.repeat)


def matrix(repeats: int = REPEATS) -> List[Scenario]:
    """Every combination in a stable order: mode, then bg, then players, then repeat."""
    out: List[Scenario] = []
    for mode in CONTROLLERS:
        for bg_label, bg_mbps in BACKGROUND:
            for players in PLAYERS:
                for repeat in range(1, repeats + 1):
                    out.append(Scenario(mode, bg_label, bg_mbps, players, repeat))
    return out


def quick_matrix() -> List[Scenario]:
    """A ~5-minute preset: one repeat, no background, 2 players, both modes."""
    return [
        Scenario("baseline", "none", 0.0, 2, 1),
        Scenario("priority", "none", 0.0, 2, 1),
    ]


def folder_for(scenario: Scenario, base: str = "results") -> str:
    """Run-folder naming: results/raw/<mode>_<bg>_<players>p_<repeat>/"""
    return os.path.join(base, "raw", scenario.folder_name)


def run_duration(scenario: Scenario) -> float:
    """Total seconds to run, including warm-up and tail."""
    return DURATION


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--list", action="store_true", help="print the matrix and exit")
    ap.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true", help="print the quick preset and exit")
    ap.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=REPEATS, help="repeats per (mode, bg, players)")
    ap.add_argument("--bg", action="append", help="add a background level: label=Mbps (repeatable)")
    ap.add_argument("--players", type=int, nargs="+", default=list(PLAYERS),
                    help="player counts to include")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if args.bg:
        extra = []
        for spec in args.bg:
            label, _, mbps = spec.partition("=")
            extra.append((label.strip(), float(mbps)))
        # not used by the default matrix; documented for custom matrices
        print("extra background levels (not in the default matrix): %s" % extra)

    if args.quick:
        for s in quick_matrix():
            print("  %-40s %s" % (s.human_name, folder_for(s)))
        return 0

    scenarios = matrix(repeats=args.repeats)
    if args.list or not (args.quick):
        print("%d scenarios (%d modes x %d bg x %d players x %d repeats)" % (
            len(scenarios), len(CONTROLLERS), len(BACKGROUND), len(PLAYERS), args.repeats))
        for s in scenarios:
            print("  %-42s -> %s  (%.0fs, %g Mbit/s bg)" % (
                s.human_name, folder_for(s), run_duration(s), s.bg_mbps))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
=== FOLDER: /topo ===
=== FILE: __init__.py ===

=== FOLDER: /topo ===
=== FILE: run_scenario.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Driver for the baseline-vs-priority experiment matrix.

Starts the right Ryu controller per mode, builds the network once per mode,
runs each scenario, appends results to results/summary.csv.

    sudo python3 -B topo/run_scenario.py --quick          # ~5 min preset
    sudo python3 -B topo/run_scenario.py --resume         # continue after interruption
    sudo python3 -B topo/run_scenario.py                  # full matrix (takes hours)

Standard library + mininet; runs in the Ubuntu VM as root.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from topo import scenarios as S
try:
    from topo import topology as T  # requires mininet (VM only)
except ImportError:
    T = None  # type: ignore[assignment]

SUMMARY_CSV = os.path.join(ROOT, "results", "summary.csv")
STATE_JSON = os.path.join(ROOT, "results", "run_state.json")

WARMUP = S.WARMUP
TAIL = S.TAIL
DURATION = S.DURATION


def parse_args():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true",
                    help="run the ~5 minute quick preset (2 scenarios, no background)")
    ap.add_argument("--resume", action="store_true",
                    help="continue from the last completed scenario in run_state.json")
    ap.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=S.REPEATS,
                    help="repeats per (mode, bg, players) for the full matrix")
    ap.add_argument("--summary", default=SUMMARY_CSV,
                    help="CSV to append results to")
    ap.add_argument("--state", default=STATE_JSON,
                    help="JSON to track progress for --resume")
    return ap.parse_args()


def load_state(state_path: str) -> dict:
    if os.path.exists(state_path):
        with open(state_path, "r") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    return {"completed": []}


def save_state(state_path: str, state: dict) -> None:
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(state_path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(state_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(state, f, indent=2)


def is_completed(state: dict, scenario: S.Scenario) -> bool:
    key = scenario.folder_name
    return key in state.get("completed", [])


def mark_completed(state: dict, scenario: S.Scenario) -> None:
    key = scenario.folder_name
    if key not in state.setdefault("completed", []):
        state["completed"].append(key)


def ensure_summary_header(path: str) -> None:
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        with open(path, "w", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(["mode", "bg_label", "bg_mbps", "players", "repeat",
                        "warmup", "tail", "duration",
                        "delay_mean_ms", "delay_p95_ms", "jitter_ms", "loss_pct",
                        "rtt_mean_ms", "rtt_p95_ms",
                        "server_delay_mean_ms", "server_delay_p95_ms", "server_jitter_ms", "server_loss_pct",
                        "folder"])


def append_summary_row(path: str, row: dict) -> None:
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "a", newline="") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow([row["mode"], row["bg_label"], row["bg_mbps"], row["players"], row["repeat"],
                    row["warmup"], row["tail"], row["duration"],
                    row["delay_mean_ms"], row["delay_p95_ms"], row["jitter_ms"], row["loss_pct"],
                    row["rtt_mean_ms"], row["rtt_p95_ms"],
                    row["server_delay_mean_ms"], row["server_delay_p95_ms"], row["server_jitter_ms"], row["server_loss_pct"],
                    row["folder"]])


def compute_aggregate_metrics(client_entries: List[dict], server_entries: List[dict]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """Pure-math aggregation of client and server metric summaries."""
    if client_entries:
        avg_delay = sum(e.get("delay_mean_ms", 0.0) for e in client_entries) / len(client_entries)
        avg_p95 = sum(e.get("delay_p95_ms", 0.0) for e in client_entries) / len(client_entries)
        avg_jitter = sum(e.get("jitter_ms", 0.0) for e in client_entries) / len(client_entries)
        avg_loss = sum(e.get("loss_pct", 0.0) for e in client_entries) / len(client_entries)

        rtt_entries = [e for e in client_entries if e.get("rtt_n")]
        if rtt_entries:
            rtt_mean = sum(e["rtt_mean_ms"] for e in rtt_entries) / len(rtt_entries)
            rtt_p95 = sum(e["rtt_p95_ms"] for e in rtt_entries) / len(rtt_entries)
        else:
            rtt_mean = rtt_p95 = 0.0
    else:
        avg_delay = avg_p95 = avg_jitter = avg_loss = rtt_mean = rtt_p95 = 0.0

    if server_entries:
        s_delay = sum(e.get("delay_mean_ms", 0.0) for e in server_entries) / len(server_entries)
        s_p95 = sum(e.get("delay_p95_ms", 0.0) for e in server_entries) / len(server_entries)
        s_jitter = sum(e.get("jitter_ms", 0.0) for e in server_entries) / len(server_entries)
        s_loss = sum(e.get("loss_pct", 0.0) for e in server_entries) / len(server_entries)
    else:
        s_delay = s_p95 = s_jitter = s_loss = 0.0

    return {
        "delay_mean_ms": round(avg_delay, 3),
        "delay_p95_ms": round(avg_p95, 3),
        "jitter_ms": round(avg_jitter, 3),
        "loss_pct": round(avg_loss, 3),
        "rtt_mean_ms": round(rtt_mean, 3),
        "rtt_p95_ms": round(rtt_p95, 3),
        "server_delay_mean_ms": round(s_delay, 3),
        "server_delay_p95_ms": round(s_p95, 3),
        "server_jitter_ms": round(s_jitter, 3),
        "server_loss_pct": round(s_loss, 3),
    }


def _find_ryu_manager() -> str:
    env_path = os.environ.get("RYU_MANAGER", "")
    for cand in (env_path,
                 os.path.expanduser("~/ryu-env/bin/ryu-manager"),
                 shutil.which("ryu-manager") or ""):
        if cand and os.path.exists(cand):
            return cand
    return os.path.expanduser("~/ryu-env/bin/ryu-manager")


def run_controller(controller_file: str, mode: str) -> subprocess.Popen:
    """Start the Ryu controller in the background."""
    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=ROOT)
    ryu = _find_ryu_manager()
    if not os.path.exists(ryu):
        sys.exit(f"ryu-manager not found at {ryu}. Set RYU_MANAGER or install: "
                 "python3 -m venv ~/ryu-env && ~/ryu-env/bin/pip install ryu eventlet")
    cmd = [ryu, os.path.join(ROOT, controller_file)]
    print(f"*** Starting {mode} controller: {' '.join(cmd)}")
    return subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)


def run_single(net, procs, scenario: S.Scenario, outdir: str, warmup: float, tail: float, duration: int) -> dict:
    """Run one scenario on an already-built network.

    Returns a dict with the summary metrics for summary.csv.
    """
    if T is None:
        raise RuntimeError("run_single requires mininet (run in the VM)")
    from analysis import metrics as M

    print(f"\n*** Running: {scenario.human_name}")
    print(f"    Output folder: {outdir}")

    # Start server
    server_proc = T.start_server(net, procs, outdir)
    time.sleep(1.0)

    # Start bots on the first N players
    bot_hosts = T.PLAYERS[:scenario.players]
    bot_procs = []
    for i, name in enumerate(bot_hosts):
        p = T.start_bot(net, procs, name, outdir, seed=i + 1, duration=duration)
        bot_procs.append(p)

    # Start background traffic if configured
    if scenario.bg_mbps > 0:
        time.sleep(1.5)  # let bots join
        print(f"    Starting background: {scenario.bg_mbps} Mbit/s UDP each way")
        T.start_background(net, procs, outdir, scenario.bg_mbps, duration - 2)

    # Wait for all bots to finish
    for p in bot_procs:
        p.wait(timeout=duration + 15)

    # Stop everything
    procs.stop_all()

    # Collect metrics with warm-up/tail exclusion
    client_entries = []
    server_entries = []
    for name in bot_hosts:
        path = os.path.join(outdir, name + ".csv")
        if os.path.exists(path):
            try:
                s = M.summarize_client(path, warmup=warmup, tail=tail)
                client_entries.append(s)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"    Warning: client {name}: {e}")

    server_path = os.path.join(outdir, "server.csv")
    if os.path.exists(server_path):
        try:
            server_map = M.summarize_server(server_path, warmup=warmup, tail=tail)
            server_entries.extend(server_map.values())
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    Warning: server: {e}")

    agg = compute_aggregate_metrics(client_entries, server_entries)

    return {
        "mode": scenario.mode,
        "bg_label": scenario.bg_label,
        "bg_mbps": scenario.bg_mbps,
        "players": scenario.players,
        "repeat": scenario.repeat,
        "warmup": warmup,
        "tail": tail,
        "duration": duration,
        "folder": os.path.basename(outdir),
        **agg
    }


def build_network(mode: str) -> tuple:
    """Build Mininet network with the correct controller for this mode."""
    controller = "none" if mode == "baseline" else "remote"
    net = T.build(controller=controller)
    procs = T.Procs()
    net.start()
    T.disable_offloads(net)
    qos_mode = "plain" if mode == "baseline" else "priority"
    T.configure_qos(net, qos_mode, T.BOTTLENECK_BPS)
    return net, procs


def run_mode(net, procs, scenarios: list, warmup: float, tail: float, duration: int,
             summary_path: str, state_path: str, state: dict) -> None:
    """Run all scenarios for one mode (baseline or priority) on the same network."""
    for scenario in scenarios:
        if is_completed(state, scenario):
            print(f"*** Skipping (already done): {scenario.human_name}")
            continue

        outdir = S.folder_for(scenario)
        row = run_single(net, procs, scenario, outdir, warmup, tail, duration)
        append_summary_row(summary_path, row)
        mark_completed(state, scenario)
        save_state(state_path, state)
        print(f"    -> delay={row['delay_mean_ms']:.2f}ms p95={row['delay_p95_ms']:.2f}ms "
              f"jitter={row['jitter_ms']:.2f}ms loss={row['loss_pct']:.2f}%")


def main() -> int:
    args = parse_args()

    if os.geteuid() != 0:
        sys.exit("Mininet needs root: run with  sudo python3 topo/run_scenario.py ...")

    # Build the list of scenarios
    if args.quick:
        scenarios = S.quick_matrix()
    else:
        scenarios = S.matrix(repeats=args.repeats)

    state = load_state(args.state)
    ensure_summary_header(args.summary)

    print(f"*** Matrix: {len(scenarios)} scenarios")
    if args.resume:
        completed = [s for s in scenarios if is_completed(state, s)]
        remaining = [s for s in scenarios if not is_completed(state, s)]
        print(f"    Completed: {len(completed)}, Remaining: {len(remaining)}")

    # Group by mode to reuse the network/controller
    by_mode = {"baseline": [], "priority": []}
    for s in scenarios:
        by_mode[s.mode].append(s)

    try:
        # Run baseline mode (wrapped in try/finally to avoid resource leaks)
        if by_mode["baseline"]:
            print(f"\n{'='*60}")
            print("*** BASELINE MODE (plain QoS, no controller)")
            print(f"{'='*60}")
            net, procs = build_network("baseline")
            try:
                run_mode(net, procs, by_mode["baseline"], WARMUP, TAIL, DURATION,
                         args.summary, args.state, state)
            finally:
                procs.stop_all()
                net.stop()

        # Run priority mode (needs Ryu controller, wrapped in try/finally)
        if by_mode["priority"]:
            print(f"\n{'='*60}")
            print("*** PRIORITY MODE (qos_switch.py + Ryu)")
            print(f"{'='*60}")
            controller = run_controller("sdn/qos_switch.py", "priority")
            time.sleep(2.0)  # give Ryu time to start

            net, procs = build_network("priority")
            try:
                run_mode(net, procs, by_mode["priority"], WARMUP, TAIL, DURATION,
                         args.summary, args.state, state)
            finally:
                procs.stop_all()
                net.stop()
                controller.terminate()
                try:
                    controller.wait(timeout=5)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    controller.kill()
                    controller.wait()

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n*** Interrupted. Run with --resume to continue.")
        save_state(args.state, state)
        return 130
    finally:
        # Clean up any stale OVS bridges (but NOT Ryu)
        if T is not None:
            T.cleanup_stale()

    print(f"\n*** All done. Summary in {args.summary}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

=== FOLDER: /topo ===
=== FILE: topology.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Mininet topology for the 4-player pong project.

                 h1  h2  h3  h4  hb1               hs (game server)   hb2
                  \\   |   |   /  /                  |               /
                   +--------- s1 ================== s2 -------------+
                                    bottleneck (10 Mbit/s, set by OVS QoS)

    h1..h4  10.0.0.1-4    players
    hb1     10.0.0.5      background-traffic source (iperf3 client)
    hs      10.0.0.10     game server
    hb2     10.0.0.11     background-traffic sink   (iperf3 server)

The only bottleneck is the s1<->s2 link, shaped in BOTH directions with an OVS
linux-htb QoS (so players -> server and server -> players are both squeezed).
All other links are unshaped.

Run from the pong/ folder, as root:

    sudo python3 topo/topology.py --smoke 15          # automated check, then exit
    sudo python3 topo/topology.py --cli --bots 3      # 3 bots + a server, you play from another tab
    sudo python3 topo/topology.py --cli               # empty network + Mininet prompt

With a controller (start Ryu FIRST, in another terminal, from the pong/ folder):
    PYTHONPATH=. ~/ryu-env/bin/ryu-manager sdn/qos_switch.py
    sudo python3 -B topo/topology.py --smoke 15 --bg-mbps 20 --controller remote --qos priority
(baseline: sdn/baseline_switch.py with --qos plain).  Do not run `mn -c` while Ryu is up:
it kills ryu-manager.  This script's own cleanup leaves it alone.

Options:
    --controller none|remote   none = switches act as plain learning switches (default)
                               remote = wait for a Ryu controller on 127.0.0.1:6653
    --qos plain|priority|none  plain    = 10 Mbit/s FIFO bottleneck (the BASELINE)
                               priority = same, plus queue 1 (game) and 2 (background)
                               none     = no shaping at all
    --datapath kernel|user     use `user` only on machines without the OVS kernel module
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from functools import partial

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(ROOT, "mininet")):
    sys.exit("ERROR: %s/mininet exists and would shadow the real mininet package.\n"
             "Rename it:  mv %s/mininet %s/topo" % (ROOT, ROOT, ROOT))

_REQUIRED = ["game/__init__.py", "game/config.py", "game/protocol.py", "game/server.py",
             "game/client.py", "analysis/metrics.py"]
_missing = [f for f in _REQUIRED if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(ROOT, f))]
if _missing:
    sys.exit("ERROR: these files are missing from %s:\n  %s\n"
             "Unzip pong_full.zip over the project (see README) and try again." % (ROOT, "\n  ".join(_missing)))

from analysis import metrics                                  # noqa: E402
from game import config as C                                  # noqa: E402

from mininet.cli import CLI                                   # noqa: E402
from mininet.link import Link                                 # noqa: E402
from mininet.log import error, info, setLogLevel              # noqa: E402
from mininet.net import Mininet                               # noqa: E402
from mininet.node import OVSSwitch, RemoteController          # noqa: E402

SERVER_IP = "10.0.0.10"
PLAYERS = ["h1", "h2", "h3", "h4"]
HOST_IPS = {"h1": "10.0.0.1", "h2": "10.0.0.2", "h3": "10.0.0.3", "h4": "10.0.0.4",
            "hb1": "10.0.0.5", "hs": SERVER_IP, "hb2": "10.0.0.11"}
PLAYER_SIDE = ["h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "hb1"]     # attached to s1
SERVER_SIDE = ["hs", "hb2"]                        # attached to s2

BOTTLENECK_BPS = 10_000_000
GAME_MIN_BPS = 4_000_000          # priority mode: guaranteed to the game queue
BG_MIN_BPS = 1_000_000            # priority mode: guaranteed to everything else
QUEUE_GAME = C.QUEUE_GAME
QUEUE_BG = C.QUEUE_BG

PY = sys.executable
ENV = dict(os.environ, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1", PYTHONUNBUFFERED="1")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- cleanup

def cleanup_stale():
    """Remove what a crashed earlier run may have left behind.

    Deliberately NOT mininet.clean.cleanup() / `mn -c`: those also `killall ryu-manager`,
    which would kill the controller you started before running this script, and delete
    every OVS bridge on the machine.  This only touches our own bridges, hosts and links.
    """
    subprocess.run(["pkill", "-9", "-f", "^bash --norc --noediting -is mininet:"], capture_output=True)  # stale host shells
    for br in ("s1", "s2"):
        subprocess.run(["ovs-vsctl", "--if-exists", "del-br", br], capture_output=True)
    links = subprocess.run(["ip", "-o", "link", "show"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    for name in set(re.findall(r"\b((?:h[1-4]|hb[12]|hs|s[12])-eth\d+)\b", links)):
        subprocess.run(["ip", "link", "del", name], capture_output=True)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- build

def build(controller="none", controller_ip="127.0.0.1", controller_port=6653, datapath="kernel"):
    fail_mode = "standalone" if controller == "none" else "secure"
    switch = partial(OVSSwitch, failMode=fail_mode, protocols="OpenFlow13", datapath=datapath)
    net = Mininet(controller=None, switch=switch, link=Link, autoSetMacs=True, waitConnected=False)
    if controller == "remote":
        net.addController("c0", controller=RemoteController, ip=controller_ip, port=controller_port)
    s1 = net.addSwitch("s1")
    s2 = net.addSwitch("s2")
    for name in PLAYER_SIDE:
        net.addLink(net.addHost(name, ip=HOST_IPS[name] + "/24"), s1)
    for name in SERVER_SIDE:
        net.addLink(net.addHost(name, ip=HOST_IPS[name] + "/24"), s2)
    net.addLink(s1, s2)
    return net


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- offloads

def disable_offloads(net):
    """Turn off TSO/GSO/GRO and checksum offload on every interface.

    Mininet's virtual links otherwise carry 64 KB "super packets" that no real link
    would, which distorts queueing at the bottleneck (and makes TCP stall on the OVS
    userspace datapath).  Needs the `ethtool` package.
    """
    if not shutil.which("ethtool"):
        error("*** WARNING: ethtool not installed (sudo apt install ethtool); offloads left on\n")
        return
    for node in net.hosts + net.switches:
        for intf in node.intfList():
            if intf.name != "lo":
                node.cmd("ethtool -K %s tso off gso off gro off tx off rx off 2>/dev/null" % intf.name)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- QoS

def bottleneck_ports(net):
    """[(switch, port name)] for both ends of the s1<->s2 link."""
    s1, s2 = net.get("s1"), net.get("s2")
    return [(s1.name, s1.connectionsTo(s2)[0][0].name),
            (s2.name, s2.connectionsTo(s1)[0][0].name)]


def _ovs(*args):
    r = subprocess.run(["ovs-vsctl"] + list(args), capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("ovs-vsctl %s failed: %s" % (" ".join(args), r.stderr.strip()))
    return r.stdout


def configure_qos(net, mode="plain", rate=BOTTLENECK_BPS):
    """Shape both ends of the bottleneck.  Safe to call again to switch modes."""
    for _sw, port in bottleneck_ports(net):
        subprocess.run(["ovs-vsctl", "clear", "port", port, "qos"], capture_output=True)  # drop old config
    if mode == "none":
        return
    for _sw, port in bottleneck_ports(net):
        if mode == "plain":
            _ovs("--", "set", "port", port, "qos=@q",
                 "--", "--id=@q", "create", "qos", "type=linux-htb", "other-config:max-rate=%d" % rate)
        elif mode == "priority":
            _ovs("--", "set", "port", port, "qos=@q",
                 "--", "--id=@q", "create", "qos", "type=linux-htb", "other-config:max-rate=%d" % rate,
                 "queues:%d=@g" % QUEUE_GAME, "queues:%d=@b" % QUEUE_BG,
                 "--", "--id=@g", "create", "queue", "other-config:min-rate=%d" % GAME_MIN_BPS,
                 "other-config:max-rate=%d" % rate, "other-config:priority=0",
                 "--", "--id=@b", "create", "queue", "other-config:min-rate=%d" % BG_MIN_BPS,
                 "other-config:max-rate=%d" % rate, "other-config:priority=1")
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown qos mode %r" % mode)
    for _sw, port in bottleneck_ports(net):          # ovs-vswitchd applies the change asynchronously
        deadline = time.time() + 5
        while "htb" not in subprocess.run(["tc", "qdisc", "show", "dev", port],
                                          capture_output=True, text=True).stdout:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                raise RuntimeError("QoS did not appear on %s (no htb qdisc after 5 s)" % port)
            time.sleep(0.2)


CLASS_LABELS = {"1:%x" % (QUEUE_GAME + 1): "queue %d (game)" % QUEUE_GAME,
                "1:%x" % (QUEUE_BG + 1): "queue %d (other)" % QUEUE_BG,
                "1:1": "queue 0 (default)",
                "1:fffe": "root (all traffic)"}


def diagnostics(net, qos, controller):
    """Print where the traffic actually went: tc class counters and the controller's flows."""
    if qos == "priority":
        print("\nPer-queue counters on the bottleneck ports (OVS linux-htb classes):")
        print("  %-6s %-24s %9s %12s %9s" % ("port", "class", "packets", "bytes", "dropped"))
        for _sw, port in bottleneck_ports(net):
            out = subprocess.run(["tc", "-s", "class", "show", "dev", port], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
            for cid, nbytes, pkts, drops in re.findall(
                    r"class htb (1:\w+) .*?\n\s*Sent (\d+) bytes (\d+) pkt \(dropped (\d+)", out):
                print("  %-6s %-24s %9s %12s %9s" % (port, CLASS_LABELS.get(cid, cid), pkts, nbytes, drops))
    if controller == "remote":
        print("\nQoS flows installed by the controller on s1:")
        out = subprocess.run(["ovs-ofctl", "-O", "OpenFlow13", "dump-flows", "s1"],
                             capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        lines = [l.strip() for l in out.splitlines() if "set_queue" in l]
        for l in lines[:8]:
            print("  " + re.sub(r"cookie=\S+ |duration=\S+ |table=\d+, |idle_age=\S+ ?|hard_age=\S+ ?|n_bytes=\S+ ", "", l))
        if not lines:
            print("  (none: this controller does not set queues)")


def measure_bottleneck(net, seconds=3):
    """TCP throughput hb1->hb2 and hb2->hb1 in Mbit/s (None if iperf3 failed)."""
    hb1, hb2 = net.get("hb1"), net.get("hb2")
    server = hb2.popen(["iperf3", "-s"], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    time.sleep(0.8)
    result = {}
    try:
        for label, extra in (("players side -> server side", ""), ("server side -> players side", "-R")):
            out = hb1.cmd("timeout %d iperf3 -c %s -t %d -J %s" % (seconds + 10, hb2.IP(), seconds, extra))
            try:
                data = json.loads(out[out.index("{"):])
                result[label] = data["end"]["sum_received"]["bits_per_second"] / 1e6
            except (ValueError, KeyError):
                result[label] = None
    finally:
        server.terminate()
        server.wait(timeout=5)
    return result


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- processes

class Procs:
    """Processes running inside Mininet hosts, with their output going to files."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.items = []

    def start(self, host, args, out_path):
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)
        out = open(out_path, "w")
        p = host.popen([str(a) for a in args], stdout=out, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, cwd=ROOT, env=ENV)
        self.items.append((p, out))
        return p

    def stop(self, proc):
        proc.terminate()                              # SIGTERM -> KeyboardInterrupt -> clean exit
        try:
            proc.wait(timeout=5)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            proc.kill()
            proc.wait()

    def stop_all(self):
        for p, out in self.items:
            if p.poll() is None:
                self.stop(p)
            out.close()
        self.items = []


def start_server(net, procs, outdir):
    return procs.start(net.get("hs"),
                       [PY, "-m", "game.server", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", C.GAME_PORT,
                        "--log", os.path.join(outdir, "server.csv"), "--stats-interval", 5],
                       os.path.join(outdir, "server.out"))


def start_bot(net, procs, name, outdir, seed, duration=0):
    return procs.start(net.get(name),
                       [PY, "-m", "game.client", "--server", SERVER_IP, "--port", C.GAME_PORT,
                        "--bot", "--headless", "--seed", seed, "--duration", duration,
                        "--log", os.path.join(outdir, name + ".csv")],
                       os.path.join(outdir, name + ".out"))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- modes

def start_background(net, procs, outdir, mbps, seconds):
    """UDP iperf3 in BOTH directions between hb1 and hb2, to saturate the bottleneck."""
    hb1, hb2 = net.get("hb1"), net.get("hb2")
    procs.start(hb2, ["iperf3", "-s"], os.path.join(outdir, "iperf_server.out"))
    time.sleep(0.5)
    procs.start(hb1, ["iperf3", "-c", hb2.IP(), "-u", "-b", "%gM" % mbps, "-t", seconds, "--bidir"],
                os.path.join(outdir, "iperf_client.out"))


def run_smoke(net, procs, seconds, outdir, bg_mbps=0.0):
    info("*** Starting game server on hs and 4 bots for %d s\n" % seconds)
    start_server(net, procs, outdir)
    time.sleep(1.0)
    bots = [start_bot(net, procs, name, outdir, seed=i + 1, duration=seconds) for i, name in enumerate(PLAYERS)]
    if bg_mbps:
        time.sleep(1.5)                           # let every bot join before the network is flooded
        info("*** Background traffic: %g Mbit/s UDP each way for %d s\n" % (bg_mbps, seconds - 2))
        start_background(net, procs, outdir, bg_mbps, seconds - 2)
    for p in bots:
        p.wait(timeout=seconds + 15)
    procs.stop_all()

    entries, ok = [], True
    for name in PLAYERS:
        path = os.path.join(outdir, name + ".csv")
        try:
            s = metrics.summarize_client(path)
        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError):
            error("no usable log for %s (see %s)\n" % (name, os.path.join(outdir, name + ".out")))
            ok = False
            continue
        entries.append((name + " (broadcasts)", s))
        if bg_mbps:                                   # congested run: loss is the result, not a failure
            ok = ok and s["n"] > 0
        elif s["n"] < 0.8 * seconds * C.TICK_HZ or s["loss_pct"] > 5:
            ok = False
    try:
        for slot, s in metrics.summarize_server(os.path.join(outdir, "server.csv")).items():
            entries.append(("server <- P%d (updates)" % (slot + 1), s))
    except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError):
        ok = False
    print("\n" + metrics.format_table(entries))
    print("\nlogs in %s" % outdir)
    return ok


def run_cli(net, procs, bots, outdir):
    start_server(net, procs, outdir)
    time.sleep(1.0)
    bot_hosts = list(reversed(PLAYERS))[:bots]
    for i, name in enumerate(bot_hosts):
        start_bot(net, procs, name, outdir, seed=i + 1)
    humans = [h for h in PLAYERS if h not in bot_hosts]
    print("\n" + "=" * 72)
    print(" Game server running on hs (%s:%d), %d bot(s) playing on %s." %
          (SERVER_IP, C.GAME_PORT, bots, ", ".join(bot_hosts) or "-"))
    if humans:
        print(" To play yourself, open ANOTHER terminal tab in the VM and run:\n")
        for h in humans:
            print("   sudo mnexec -a %d bash -c 'cd %s && PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 "
                  "python3 -m game.client --server %s'    # %s" % (net.get(h).pid, ROOT, SERVER_IP, h))
        print("\n (add --bot to that command if you want a bot instead; W/S or A/D to move, Q to quit)")
    print(" This terminal is now the Mininet prompt: try  pingall  or  hb1 iperf3 -c 10.0.0.11 -t 5")
    print(" Type  exit  to stop everything.")
    print("=" * 72 + "\n")
    CLI(net)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    mode = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    mode.add_argument("--smoke", type=int, metavar="SECONDS", help="automated check of the whole setup")
    mode.add_argument("--cli", action="store_true", help="start the network and open the Mininet prompt")
    ap.add_argument("--bg-mbps", type=float, default=0.0,
                    help="(--smoke) add UDP background traffic of this rate in each direction")
    ap.add_argument("--bots", type=int, default=0, choices=range(0, 5), help="(--cli) bots to start, 0-4")
    ap.add_argument("--controller", choices=("none", "remote"), default="none")
    ap.add_argument("--controller-ip", default="127.0.0.1")
    ap.add_argument("--controller-port", type=int, default=6653)
    ap.add_argument("--qos", choices=("plain", "priority", "none"), default="plain")
    ap.add_argument("--rate-mbps", type=float, default=BOTTLENECK_BPS / 1e6)
    ap.add_argument("--datapath", choices=("kernel", "user"), default="kernel")
    ap.add_argument("--out", help="folder for logs (default results/raw/<mode>)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if os.geteuid() != 0:
        sys.exit("Mininet needs root: run with  sudo python3 topo/topology.py ...")
    setLogLevel("info")
    rate = int(args.rate_mbps * 1e6)
    outdir = args.out or os.path.join(ROOT, "results", "raw", "smoke" if args.smoke else "demo")

    cleanup_stale()                               # leftovers from a crashed earlier run (keeps Ryu alive)
    net = build(args.controller, args.controller_ip, args.controller_port, args.datapath)
    procs = Procs()
    status = 0
    try:
        net.start()
        if args.controller == "remote" and not net.waitConnected(timeout=20):
            error("*** Switches never connected to %s:%d - start Ryu first "
                  "(~/ryu-env/bin/ryu-manager ...)\n" % (args.controller_ip, args.controller_port))
            return 1
        disable_offloads(net)
        configure_qos(net, args.qos, rate)
        info("*** QoS mode: %s (%s)\n" % (args.qos, "%.1f Mbit/s" % (rate / 1e6) if args.qos != "none" else "unshaped"))

        if args.smoke:
            info("*** Connectivity\n")
            drop = net.pingAll()
            info("*** Bottleneck throughput (configured %.1f Mbit/s)\n" % (rate / 1e6))
            tput = measure_bottleneck(net)
            for label, mbps in tput.items():
                print("    %-30s %s" % (label, "%.2f Mbit/s" % mbps if mbps is not None else "iperf3 failed"))
            game_ok = run_smoke(net, procs, args.smoke, outdir, args.bg_mbps)
            diagnostics(net, args.qos, args.controller)
            shaped_ok = args.qos == "none" or all(
                m is not None and 0.6 * rate / 1e6 <= m <= 1.05 * rate / 1e6 for m in tput.values())
            print("\nRESULT: ping %s | bottleneck %s | game %s" % (
                "OK" if drop == 0 else "FAILED (%.0f%% dropped)" % drop,
                "OK" if shaped_ok else "NOT AT CONFIGURED RATE",
                "OK" if game_ok else "FAILED"))
            status = 0 if (drop == 0 and shaped_ok and game_ok) else 1
        else:
            run_cli(net, procs, args.bots, outdir)
    finally:
        procs.stop_all()
        net.stop()
    return status


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

=== FOLDER: /sdn ===
=== FILE: baseline_switch.py ===
"""BASELINE controller: a plain OpenFlow 1.3 L2 learning switch.

This is the control for every experiment: it forwards everything the same way and
knows nothing about the game.  qos_switch.py subclasses it, so the two controllers
forward identically and differ ONLY in queue selection - a fair comparison.

Runs under the Ryu environment (Python 3.9): keep the syntax 3.9-compatible.

    cd ~/CN_project/pong
    PYTHONPATH=. ~/ryu-env/bin/ryu-manager sdn/baseline_switch.py
"""
from ryu.base import app_manager
from ryu.controller import ofp_event
from ryu.controller.handler import CONFIG_DISPATCHER, MAIN_DISPATCHER, set_ev_cls
from ryu.lib.packet import ether_types, ethernet, packet
from ryu.ofproto import ofproto_v1_3

PRIO_TABLE_MISS = 0
PRIO_FORWARD = 10


class BaselineSwitch(app_manager.RyuApp):
    OFP_VERSIONS = [ofproto_v1_3.OFP_VERSION]

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super(BaselineSwitch, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.mac_to_port = {}                 # dpid -> {mac: port}

    # --- switch connects: send unknown packets to us -----------------------------

    @set_ev_cls(ofp_event.EventOFPSwitchFeatures, CONFIG_DISPATCHER)
    def _on_features(self, ev):
        dp = ev.msg.datapath
        ofp, parser = dp.ofproto, dp.ofproto_parser
        actions = [parser.OFPActionOutput(ofp.OFPP_CONTROLLER, ofp.OFPCML_NO_BUFFER)]
        self.add_flow(dp, PRIO_TABLE_MISS, parser.OFPMatch(), actions)
        self.logger.info("switch %016x connected (%s)", dp.id, type(self).__name__)

    # --- learning ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    @set_ev_cls(ofp_event.EventOFPPacketIn, MAIN_DISPATCHER)
    def _on_packet_in(self, ev):
        msg = ev.msg
        dp = msg.datapath
        ofp, parser = dp.ofproto, dp.ofproto_parser
        in_port = msg.match["in_port"]

        eth = packet.Packet(msg.data).get_protocols(ethernet.ethernet)[0]
        if eth.ethertype == ether_types.ETH_TYPE_LLDP:
            return
        table = self.mac_to_port.setdefault(dp.id, {})
        table[eth.src] = in_port
        out_port = table.get(eth.dst, ofp.OFPP_FLOOD)

        if out_port != ofp.OFPP_FLOOD:
            self.install_forwarding(dp, eth.dst, out_port)

        data = msg.data if msg.buffer_id == ofp.OFP_NO_BUFFER else None
        dp.send_msg(parser.OFPPacketOut(datapath=dp, buffer_id=msg.buffer_id, in_port=in_port,
                                        actions=[parser.OFPActionOutput(out_port)], data=data))

    # --- the one thing subclasses change ------------------------------------------

    def install_forwarding(self, dp, dst_mac, out_port):
        """Called once we know which port `dst_mac` lives behind."""
        parser = dp.ofproto_parser
        self.add_flow(dp, PRIO_FORWARD, parser.OFPMatch(eth_dst=dst_mac),
                      [parser.OFPActionOutput(out_port)])

    def add_flow(self, dp, priority, match, actions, idle_timeout=0):
        ofp, parser = dp.ofproto, dp.ofproto_parser
        inst = [parser.OFPInstructionActions(ofp.OFPIT_APPLY_ACTIONS, actions)]
        dp.send_msg(parser.OFPFlowMod(datapath=dp, priority=priority, match=match,
                                      instructions=inst, idle_timeout=idle_timeout))

=== FOLDER: /sdn ===
=== FILE: __init__.py ===

=== FOLDER: /sdn ===
=== FILE: qos_switch.py ===
"""SDN controller that prioritises the game.

Forwards exactly like baseline_switch.py, but for every destination it also installs:

    priority 20   UDP port 5000 (either direction)   ->  set_queue 1 (game)   + output
    priority 10   everything else                    ->  set_queue 2 (other)  + output

The queues exist on the two bottleneck ports (OVS QoS, created by
`topo/topology.py --qos priority`): queue 1 is the high-priority class with a bandwidth
guarantee, queue 2 the low-priority one.  Ports without those queues ignore set_queue.

Runs under the Ryu environment (Python 3.9):

    cd ~/CN_project/pong
    PYTHONPATH=. ~/ryu-env/bin/ryu-manager sdn/qos_switch.py
"""
from game import config as C
from sdn.baseline_switch import PRIO_FORWARD, BaselineSwitch

PRIO_GAME = 20
ETH_TYPE_IP = 0x0800
IP_PROTO_UDP = 17


class QosSwitch(BaselineSwitch):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super(QosSwitch, self).__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.logger.info("QoS: UDP port %d -> queue %d, everything else -> queue %d",
                         C.GAME_PORT, C.QUEUE_GAME, C.QUEUE_BG)

    def install_forwarding(self, dp, dst_mac, out_port):
        parser = dp.ofproto_parser
        output = parser.OFPActionOutput(out_port)

        # everything else: low-priority queue
        self.add_flow(dp, PRIO_FORWARD, parser.OFPMatch(eth_dst=dst_mac),
                      [parser.OFPActionSetQueue(C.QUEUE_BG), output])

        # game traffic, both directions (client -> server uses dst port, server -> client src port)
        for field in ("udp_dst", "udp_src"):
            match = parser.OFPMatch(eth_dst=dst_mac, eth_type=ETH_TYPE_IP, ip_proto=IP_PROTO_UDP,
                                    **{field: C.GAME_PORT})
            self.add_flow(dp, PRIO_GAME, match, [parser.OFPActionSetQueue(C.QUEUE_GAME), output])
        self.logger.info("switch %016x: %s is behind port %d (game -> queue %d)",
                         dp.id, dst_mac, out_port, C.QUEUE_GAME)

=== FOLDER: /tests ===
=== FILE: test_protocol.py ===
import unittest

from game import protocol as p
from game.seq import DUPLICATE, LATE, NEW, STALE, SeqTracker


class ProtocolTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_header_roundtrip_every_type(self):
        payloads = {
            p.JOIN: b"", p.JOIN_ACK: b"", p.LEAVE: b"", p.PING: b"",
            p.STATE_UPDATE: p.pack_update(-1),
            p.PONG: p.pack_echo(12.5),
            p.STATE_BROADCAST: p.pack_broadcast(p.Snapshot(
                1, p.PHASE_PLAYING, .5, .5, (.5,) * 4, (3,) * 4, (1,) * 4)),
        }
        for msg_type, payload in payloads.items():
            data = p.encode(msg_type, 77, 2, payload, send_time=123.456)
            hdr, body = p.decode(data)
            self.assertEqual(hdr.msg_type, msg_type)
            self.assertEqual(hdr.seq, 77)
            self.assertEqual(hdr.player_id, 2)
            self.assertEqual(hdr.send_time, 123.456)
            self.assertEqual(body, payload)

    def test_header_size(self):
        self.assertEqual(p.HEADER.size, 15)

    def test_short_packet_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(p.ProtocolError):
            p.decode(b"\x01\x02")
        with self.assertRaises(p.ProtocolError):
            p.decode(b"")

    def test_wrong_version_rejected(self):
        data = bytearray(p.encode(p.JOIN, 0))
        data[0] = 99
        with self.assertRaises(p.ProtocolError):
            p.decode(bytes(data))

    def test_unknown_type_rejected(self):
        data = bytearray(p.encode(p.JOIN, 0))
        data[1] = 200
        with self.assertRaises(p.ProtocolError):
            p.decode(bytes(data))

    def test_wrong_payload_size_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(p.ProtocolError):
            p.decode(p.encode(p.STATE_UPDATE, 0, 0, b"\x00\x00"))
        with self.assertRaises(p.ProtocolError):
            p.decode(p.encode(p.JOIN, 0, 0, b"junk"))
        with self.assertRaises(p.ProtocolError):
            p.decode(p.encode(p.STATE_BROADCAST, 0, 0, b"\x00" * 5))

    def test_random_garbage_never_crashes(self):
        import os
        for _ in range(500):
            junk = os.urandom(int.from_bytes(os.urandom(1), "big") % 80)
            try:
                p.decode(junk)
            except p.ProtocolError:
                pass

    def test_update_is_clamped(self):
        self.assertEqual(p.unpack_update(p.pack_update(5)), 1)
        self.assertEqual(p.unpack_update(p.pack_update(-9)), -1)
        self.assertEqual(p.unpack_update(p.pack_update(0)), 0)

    def test_broadcast_roundtrip(self):
        snap = p.Snapshot(tick=12345, phase=p.PHASE_GAME_OVER, ball_x=0.25, ball_y=0.75,
                          paddles=(0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.9), lives=(3, 0, 1, 2), active=(1, 1, 0, 1))
        out = p.unpack_broadcast(p.pack_broadcast(snap))
        self.assertEqual(out.tick, 12345)
        self.assertEqual(out.phase, p.PHASE_GAME_OVER)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out.ball_x, 0.25, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out.ball_y, 0.75, places=6)
        for a, b in zip(out.paddles, snap.paddles):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(a, b, places=6)
        self.assertEqual(out.lives, snap.lives)
        self.assertEqual(out.active, snap.active)

    def test_seq_wraps_to_32_bits(self):
        hdr, _ = p.decode(p.encode(p.JOIN, 2 ** 32 + 5))
        self.assertEqual(hdr.seq, 5)


class SeqTrackerTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_in_order_has_no_loss(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        for s in range(100):
            self.assertEqual(t.observe(s), (NEW, 0))
        self.assertEqual((t.expected, t.received, t.lost), (100, 100, 0))

    def test_first_packet_can_be_mid_stream(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(5000), (NEW, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.lost, 0)
        self.assertEqual(t.expected, 1)

    def test_gap_is_reported(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        t.observe(0)
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(4), (NEW, 3))
        self.assertEqual(t.lost, 3)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(t.loss_ratio, 3 / 5)

    def test_duplicate_ignored(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        t.observe(0)
        t.observe(1)
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(1), (DUPLICATE, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(0), (DUPLICATE, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.duplicates, 2)
        self.assertEqual(t.received, 2)

    def test_late_packet_fills_gap_and_reduces_loss(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        t.observe(0)
        t.observe(3)
        self.assertEqual(t.lost, 2)
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(2), (LATE, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.lost, 1)
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(2), (DUPLICATE, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(1), (LATE, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.lost, 0)
        self.assertEqual(t.late, 2)

    def test_stale_packet_ignored(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        t.observe(0)
        t.observe(200)
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(1), (STALE, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.stale, 1)

    def test_huge_jump_is_cheap_and_correct(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        t.observe(0)
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(2 ** 31), (NEW, 2 ** 31 - 1))
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(2 ** 31), (DUPLICATE, 0))

    def test_reset(self):
        t = SeqTracker()
        t.observe(0)
        t.observe(9)
        t.reset()
        self.assertEqual(t.observe(0), (NEW, 0))
        self.assertEqual(t.lost, 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

=== FOLDER: /tests ===
=== FILE: test_loopback.py ===
"""End-to-end tests: real UDP sockets on 127.0.0.1, server and clients in one process."""
import os
import socket
import time
import unittest

from game import protocol as P
from game.bot import Bot
from game.client import GameClient, JoinError
from game.server import GameServer

LOCAL = "127.0.0.1"


class LoopbackTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.server = None
        self.clients = []

    def tearDown(self):
        for c in self.clients:
            c.leave()
        if self.server:
            self.server.stop()

    def make_server(self, **kw):
        self.server = GameServer(LOCAL, 0, seed=1, **kw)
        self.server.start()
        return self.server

    def make_client(self, start=True, **kw):
        kw.setdefault("input_fn", Bot(seed=len(self.clients)))
        c = GameClient(LOCAL, self.server.port, **kw)
        self.clients.append(c)
        c.join()
        if start:
            c.start()
        return c

    def test_four_bots_play_and_agree(self):
        self.make_server()
        clients = [self.make_client() for _ in range(4)]
        self.assertEqual(sorted(c.player_id for c in clients), [0, 1, 2, 3])
        time.sleep(2.5)
        snaps = [c.get_snapshot() for c in clients]
        self.assertTrue(all(s is not None for s in snaps))
        self.assertEqual(snaps[0].phase, P.PHASE_PLAYING)
        ticks = [s.tick for s in snaps]
        self.assertLessEqual(max(ticks) - min(ticks), 5, "clients should see (almost) the same tick")
        for c in clients:
            st = c.stats()
            self.assertGreater(st["received"], 40)
            self.assertLess(st["loss_ratio"], 0.05)       # loopback: effectively lossless
            self.assertIsNotNone(st["rtt"])                # PING/PONG works
        self.assertEqual(self.server.stats()["players"], 4)
        for c in clients:
            c.leave()
        time.sleep(0.4)
        self.assertEqual(self.server.stats()["players"], 0)

    def test_fifth_player_is_rejected(self):
        self.make_server()
        for _ in range(4):
            self.make_client(start=False)
        extra = GameClient(LOCAL, self.server.port)
        self.clients.append(extra)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(JoinError, "full"):
            extra.join()

    def test_duplicate_join_gets_same_slot(self):
        self.make_server()
        c = self.make_client(start=False)
        again = c.join()                    # a retried JOIN must not consume a second slot
        self.assertEqual(again, c.player_id)
        self.assertEqual(self.server.stats()["players"], 1)

    def test_join_retries_then_gives_up(self):
        silent = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        silent.bind((LOCAL, 0))
        try:
            c = GameClient(LOCAL, silent.getsockname()[1], join_retries=3, join_timeout=0.1)
            self.clients.append(c)
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(JoinError, "no reply"):
                c.join()
            self.assertGreaterEqual(time.monotonic() - t0, 0.28)
            silent.settimeout(0.3)
            joins = 0
            try:
                while True:
                    P.decode(silent.recvfrom(100)[0])
                    joins += 1
            except socket.timeout:
                pass
            self.assertEqual(joins, 3)           # JOIN was retried exactly 3 times
        finally:
            silent.close()

    def test_packet_loss_is_detected_and_game_continues(self):
        self.make_server()
        lossy = self.make_client(drop_prob=0.3, loss_seed=1)
        self.make_client()
        time.sleep(3)
        st = lossy.stats()
        self.assertGreater(st["lost"], 0)
        self.assertGreater(lossy.dropped, 0)
        self.assertGreater(st["loss_ratio"], 0.1)       # ~30% injected
        self.assertLess(st["loss_ratio"], 0.5)
        self.assertLess(lossy.seconds_since_data(), 1.0)  # still receiving fresh state
        self.assertEqual(lossy.get_snapshot().phase, P.PHASE_PLAYING)

    def test_silent_player_times_out(self):
        self.make_server(player_timeout=0.5)
        self.make_client(start=False)       # joins, then never sends anything
        self.assertEqual(self.server.stats()["players"], 1)
        time.sleep(1.2)
        self.assertEqual(self.server.stats()["players"], 0)

    def test_server_survives_garbage(self):
        self.make_server()
        raw = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        for _ in range(50):
            raw.sendto(os.urandom(30), (LOCAL, self.server.port))
        raw.sendto(b"", (LOCAL, self.server.port))
        raw.close()
        time.sleep(0.3)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.server.stats()["bad_packets"], 40)
        c = self.make_client(start=False)    # still serving normally
        self.assertIsNotNone(c.player_id)

    def test_logs_are_written(self):
        import csv
        import tempfile
        d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.make_server(log_path=os.path.join(d, "server.csv"))
        c = self.make_client(log_path=os.path.join(d, "c.csv"))
        time.sleep(1.5)
        c.leave()
        self.server.stop()
        with open(os.path.join(d, "c.csv")) as f:
            crows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
        with open(os.path.join(d, "server.csv")) as f:
            srows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
        self.assertGreater(len([r for r in crows if r["kind"] == "new"]), 20)
        self.assertGreater(len(srows), 20)
        r = crows[0]
        self.assertGreaterEqual(float(r["recv_time"]) - float(r["send_time"]), 0)   # one-way delay >= 0
        self.server = None


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

=== FOLDER: /tests ===
=== FILE: test_metrics.py ===
import csv
import os
import tempfile
import unittest

from analysis import metrics as M


def rows(seqs, delay_ms=5.0, kind="new"):
    """Packets sent at t = seq * 0.01 s that each took `delay_ms` (a number or a list)."""
    out = []
    for i, s in enumerate(seqs):
        d = delay_ms[i % len(delay_ms)] if isinstance(delay_ms, list) else delay_ms
        out.append((kind, s * 0.01 + d / 1000.0, s, s * 0.01))
    return out


class MetricsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_clean_stream(self):
        s = M.summarize(rows(range(100)))
        self.assertEqual((s["n"], s["expected"], s["lost"]), (100, 100, 0))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["delay_mean_ms"], 5.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["jitter_ms"], 0.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["delay_p95_ms"], 5.0, places=6)

    def test_loss_from_gaps(self):
        s = M.summarize(rows([s for s in range(10) if s not in (3, 4)]))
        self.assertEqual((s["expected"], s["lost"]), (10, 2))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["loss_pct"], 20.0)

    def test_late_counts_as_received_duplicates_ignored(self):
        data = rows([0, 1, 3]) + rows([2], kind="late") + rows([1], kind="duplicate")
        s = M.summarize(data)
        self.assertEqual((s["n"], s["expected"], s["lost"]), (4, 4, 0))

    def test_jitter_of_alternating_delay(self):
        s = M.summarize(rows(range(50), delay_ms=[5.0, 15.0]))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["jitter_ms"], 10.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["delay_mean_ms"], 10.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["delay_max_ms"], 15.0, places=6)

    def test_percentile(self):
        s = M.summarize(rows(range(101), delay_ms=[float(i) for i in range(101)]))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["delay_p50_ms"], 50.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s["delay_p95_ms"], 95.0, places=6)

    def test_empty(self):
        s = M.summarize([])
        self.assertEqual((s["n"], s["expected"], s["loss_pct"], s["jitter_ms"]), (0, 0, 0.0, 0.0))

    def test_client_and_server_csv(self):
        d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        cp, sp = os.path.join(d, "c.csv"), os.path.join(d, "s.csv")
        with open(cp, "w", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(["kind", "recv_time", "seq", "send_time"])
            for kind, recv, seq, send in rows([0, 1, 3]):
                w.writerow([kind, recv, seq, send])
            w.writerow(["pong", 10.020, 0, 10.000])        # 20 ms RTT
        with open(sp, "w", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(["recv_time", "player", "seq", "send_time", "kind"])
            for kind, recv, seq, send in rows(range(5)):
                w.writerow([recv, 2, seq, send, kind])
        c = M.summarize_client(cp)
        self.assertEqual((c["n"], c["lost"], c["rtt_n"]), (3, 1, 1))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(c["rtt_mean_ms"], 20.0, places=3)
        s = M.summarize_server(sp)
        self.assertEqual(list(s), [2])
        self.assertEqual(s[2]["n"], 5)
        text = M.format_table([("client", c)])
        self.assertIn("client", text)
        self.assertEqual(M.main([cp, sp]), 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
