=== FILE: test_run_scenario.py ===
import os
import tempfile
import unittest

from topo import run_scenario as RS
from topo import scenarios as S


class RunScenarioStateTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmpdir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.state_file = os.path.join(self.tmpdir, "state.json")
        self.summary_file = os.path.join(self.tmpdir, "summary.csv")

    def test_load_save_state(self):
        state = RS.load_state(self.state_file)
        self.assertEqual(state, {"completed": []})

        scenario = S.Scenario("baseline", "none", 0.0, 2, 1)
        self.assertFalse(RS.is_completed(state, scenario))

        RS.mark_completed(state, scenario)
        self.assertTrue(RS.is_completed(state, scenario))

        RS.save_state(self.state_file, state)
        loaded = RS.load_state(self.state_file)
        self.assertTrue(RS.is_completed(loaded, scenario))

    def test_ensure_summary_header_and_append_row(self):
        RS.ensure_summary_header(self.summary_file)
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(self.summary_file))

        row = {
            "mode": "baseline",
            "bg_label": "none",
            "bg_mbps": 0.0,
            "players": 2,
            "repeat": 1,
            "warmup": 2.0,
            "tail": 1.0,
            "duration": 15,
            "delay_mean_ms": 1.23,
            "delay_p95_ms": 2.34,
            "jitter_ms": 0.45,
            "loss_pct": 0.0,
            "rtt_mean_ms": 5.67,
            "rtt_p95_ms": 6.78,
            "server_delay_mean_ms": 1.11,
            "server_delay_p95_ms": 2.22,
            "server_jitter_ms": 0.33,
            "server_loss_pct": 0.0,
            "folder": "baseline_none_2p_r1",
        }
        RS.append_summary_row(self.summary_file, row)

        with open(self.summary_file, "r") as f:
            lines = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]
        self.assertEqual(len(lines), 2)
        self.assertIn("delay_mean_ms", lines[0])
        self.assertIn("baseline_none_2p_r1", lines[1])

    def test_pure_math_aggregation(self):
        client_entries = [
            {
                "delay_mean_ms": 10.0,
                "delay_p95_ms": 15.0,
                "jitter_ms": 2.0,
                "loss_pct": 1.0,
                "rtt_n": 5,
                "rtt_mean_ms": 20.0,
                "rtt_p95_ms": 25.0,
            },
            {
                "delay_mean_ms": 20.0,
                "delay_p95_ms": 25.0,
                "jitter_ms": 4.0,
                "loss_pct": 3.0,
                "rtt_n": 0,
                "rtt_mean_ms": 0.0,
                "rtt_p95_ms": 0.0,
            },
        ]
        server_entries = [
            {
                "delay_mean_ms": 5.0,
                "delay_p95_ms": 8.0,
                "jitter_ms": 1.0,
                "loss_pct": 0.5,
            }
        ]

        agg = RS.compute_aggregate_metrics(client_entries, server_entries)
        self.assertEqual(agg["delay_mean_ms"], 15.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["delay_p95_ms"], 20.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["jitter_ms"], 3.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["loss_pct"], 2.0)
        # RTT should only average over entries where rtt_n > 0
        self.assertEqual(agg["rtt_mean_ms"], 20.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["rtt_p95_ms"], 25.0)

        self.assertEqual(agg["server_delay_mean_ms"], 5.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["server_delay_p95_ms"], 8.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["server_jitter_ms"], 1.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["server_loss_pct"], 0.5)

    def test_pure_math_empty_entries(self):
        agg = RS.compute_aggregate_metrics([], [])
        self.assertEqual(agg["delay_mean_ms"], 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["rtt_mean_ms"], 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(agg["server_delay_mean_ms"], 0.0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import unittest
import tempfile
import os

class GraphTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_png_exists_for_all_four(self):
        # The test data already created PNGs; just verify files exist
        # In real use, graphs.py creates them when run with real aggregate.csv
        # We test the module imports correctly here.
        try:
            import analysis.graphs as G
            self.assertTrue(hasattr(G, "plot_delay_by_bg"))
            self.assertTrue(hasattr(G, "main"))
        except ImportError:
            self.skipTest("matplotlib not installed")

    def test_plot_functions_run_without_crash(self):
        try:
            import analysis.graphs as G
            import csv, tempfile
            d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            p = os.path.join(d, "agg.csv")
            with open(p, "w", newline="") as f:
                w = csv.writer(f)
                w.writerow(["mode","bg_label","players","bg_mbps","metric","mean","std","n"])
                w.writerow(["baseline","none",2,0,"delay_mean_ms",5,0.1,3])
            G.main([p, "--out-dir", d])
            for name in ("delay_by_bg.png", "jitter_by_bg.png", "loss_by_bg.png", "delay_vs_players.png"):
                self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(d, name)), name)
        except ImportError:
            self.skipTest("matplotlib not installed")

=== FOLDER: /tests ===
=== FILE: test_ui.py ===
import unittest
from unittest import mock

from game import config as C
from game import protocol as P
from game import tui
from game.tui import (BALL, BG, P1, P2, P3, P4, TRAIL1, WALL, Glyphs, HumanInput, Layout, key_help,
                      render_pixels, to_cells)


def snap(active=(1, 1, 0, 0), lives=(3, 3, 3, 3), paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5), ball=(0.5, 0.5),
         phase=P.PHASE_PLAYING):
    return P.Snapshot(tick=1, phase=phase, ball_x=ball[0], ball_y=ball[1], paddles=paddles,
                      lives=lives, active=active)


def pixels(buf, color):
    return {(x, y) for y, row in enumerate(buf) for x, c in enumerate(row) if c == color}


class LayoutTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_default_terminal(self):
        lay = Layout(24, 80)
        self.assertTrue(lay.ok)
        self.assertEqual(lay.N, 2 * lay.R)
        self.assertEqual(lay.R, 18)                       # 24 rows - 6 fixed rows
        self.assertFalse(lay.boxed_footer)
        self.assertLessEqual(lay.footer_y, 23)

    def test_big_terminal_has_boxed_footer_and_is_capped(self):
        lay = Layout(60, 200)
        self.assertTrue(lay.boxed_footer)
        self.assertEqual(lay.N, tui.MAX_SIDE)
        self.assertLessEqual(lay.footer_y + 2, 59)

    def test_everything_fits_on_screen(self):
        for rows in range(17, 70):
            for cols in (50, 64, 80, 120):
                lay = Layout(rows, cols)
                self.assertTrue(lay.ok, (rows, cols))
                self.assertLessEqual(lay.x0 + lay.N + 2, cols)
                last = lay.footer_y + (2 if lay.boxed_footer else 0)
                self.assertLessEqual(last, rows - 1, (rows, cols))
                self.assertEqual(lay.N % 2, 0)

    def test_too_small(self):
        self.assertFalse(Layout(10, 80).ok)
        self.assertFalse(Layout(40, 30).ok)
        self.assertFalse(Layout(0, 0).ok)


class RenderTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_paddles_sit_on_their_own_walls(self):
        side = 36
        buf = render_pixels(snap(active=(1, 1, 1, 1)), side)
        for color, in_wall in ((P1, lambda x, y: x < 2), (P2, lambda x, y: x >= side - 2),
                               (P3, lambda x, y: y < 2), (P4, lambda x, y: y >= side - 2)):
            px = pixels(buf, color)
            self.assertTrue(px, color)
            self.assertTrue(all(in_wall(x, y) for x, y in px), color)

    def test_paddle_is_centred_and_about_a_fifth_of_the_wall(self):
        side = 40
        buf = render_pixels(snap(paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5)), side)
        ys = sorted({y for _, y in pixels(buf, P1)})
        self.assertEqual(len(ys), round(C.PADDLE_LEN * side))
        self.assertAlmostEqual((ys[0] + ys[-1]) / 2, (side - 1) / 2, delta=0.6)

    def test_paddle_follows_position_and_stays_inside(self):
        side = 36
        for p in (C.PADDLE_LEN / 2, 0.3, 1 - C.PADDLE_LEN / 2):
            buf = render_pixels(snap(paddles=(p, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5)), side)
            ys = {y for _, y in pixels(buf, P1)}
            self.assertTrue(ys)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(min(ys), 0)
            self.assertLessEqual(max(ys), side - 1)

    def test_empty_and_eliminated_walls_are_solid(self):
        side = 36
        buf = render_pixels(snap(active=(1, 1, 1, 0), lives=(3, 3, 0, 3)), side)
        wall = pixels(buf, WALL)
        # slot 2 is active but out of lives, slot 3 is empty: both walls are solid, full length
        self.assertTrue(all((x, 0) in wall for x in range(side)))
        self.assertTrue(all((x, side - 1) in wall for x in range(side)))
        self.assertFalse(pixels(buf, P3))
        self.assertFalse(pixels(buf, P4))

    def test_ball_is_a_two_by_two_square_and_only_while_playing(self):
        side = 36
        buf = render_pixels(snap(ball=(0.5, 0.5)), side)
        b = pixels(buf, BALL)
        self.assertEqual(len(b), 4)
        xs, ys = {x for x, _ in b}, {y for _, y in b}
        self.assertEqual((max(xs) - min(xs), max(ys) - min(ys)), (1, 1))
        for phase in (P.PHASE_WAITING, P.PHASE_GAME_OVER):
            self.assertFalse(pixels(render_pixels(snap(phase=phase), side), BALL))
        self.assertFalse(pixels(render_pixels(snap(), side, show_ball=False), BALL))

    def test_ball_never_leaves_the_arena(self):
        side = 36
        for bx, by in ((0, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (1, 0), (-0.5, 2.0)):
            buf = render_pixels(snap(ball=(bx, by)), side)
            self.assertEqual(len(pixels(buf, BALL)), 4, (bx, by))

    def test_trail_is_drawn_dim_and_never_over_other_things(self):
        side = 36
        buf = render_pixels(snap(ball=(0.5, 0.5)), side, trail=[(0.2, 0.2), (0.3, 0.3)])
        self.assertIn((int(0.3 * side), int(0.3 * side)), pixels(buf, TRAIL1))
        # a trail dot on top of a paddle must not erase the paddle
        buf = render_pixels(snap(paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5)), side, trail=[(0.01, 0.5)])
        self.assertEqual(buf[int(0.5 * side)][0], P1)

    def test_no_snapshot_draws_four_solid_walls(self):
        side = 30
        buf = render_pixels(None, side)
        self.assertEqual(len(pixels(buf, WALL)),
                         len({(x, y) for y in range(side) for x in range(side)
                              if x < 2 or y < 2 or x >= side - 2 or y >= side - 2}))
        self.assertFalse(pixels(buf, BALL))


class CellTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def grid(self, top, bottom):
        return [list(top), list(bottom)]

    def test_each_combination(self):
        runs = to_cells(self.grid([BG, P1, BG, P1, P1], [BG, BG, P1, P1, P2]))[0]
        cells = {}                                    # expand runs back to one entry per cell
        for x, text, fg, bg in runs:
            for k, ch in enumerate(text):
                cells[x + k] = (ch, fg, bg)
        self.assertEqual(cells[1], ("\u2580", P1, BG))          # top only
        self.assertEqual(cells[2], ("\u2584", P1, BG))          # bottom only
        self.assertEqual(cells[3], ("\u2588", P1, BG))          # both, same colour
        self.assertEqual(cells[4], ("\u2580", P1, P2))          # both, different colours
        self.assertNotIn(0, cells)                               # empty cell is skipped
        # cells 1-3 share one colour pair, so they are written with a single call
        self.assertEqual(runs[0], (1, "\u2580\u2584\u2588", P1, BG))

    def test_equal_neighbours_merge_into_runs(self):
        runs = to_cells(self.grid([P1] * 5, [P1] * 5))[0]
        self.assertEqual(runs, [(0, "█" * 5, P1, BG)])
        runs = to_cells(self.grid([P1, P1, BG, P1], [P1, P1, BG, P1]))[0]
        self.assertEqual([(x, len(t)) for x, t, *_ in runs], [(0, 2), (3, 1)])

    def test_ascii_fallback(self):
        runs = to_cells(self.grid([P1, BG], [BG, P2]), unicode_ok=False)[0]
        self.assertEqual(runs, [(0, "#", P1, BG), (1, "#", P2, BG)])

    def test_every_cell_row_of_a_real_frame(self):
        buf = render_pixels(snap(active=(1, 1, 1, 1)), 36, [(0.4, 0.4)])
        rows = to_cells(buf)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 18)
        for runs in rows:
            for x, text, fg, bg in runs:
                self.assertTrue(0 <= x and x + len(text) <= 36)


class InputTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_keys(self):
        import curses
        self.assertEqual(tui.key_to_direction(ord("w"), 0), -1)
        self.assertEqual(tui.key_to_direction(curses.KEY_DOWN, 1), 1)
        self.assertEqual(tui.key_to_direction(ord("a"), 2), -1)
        self.assertEqual(tui.key_to_direction(curses.KEY_RIGHT, 3), 1)
        self.assertEqual(tui.key_to_direction(ord(" "), 0), 0)
        self.assertIsNone(tui.key_to_direction(ord("w"), 2))     # wrong axis for a top-wall player
        self.assertIsNone(tui.key_to_direction(ord("x"), 0))

    def test_tap_mode_expires(self):
        h = HumanInput(sticky=False)
        with mock.patch("game.tui.time.monotonic", side_effect=[100.0, 100.05, 100.5]):
            h.press(1)                    # 100.0 -> held until 100.2
            self.assertEqual(h(None, 0), 1)      # 100.05
            self.assertEqual(h(None, 0), 0)      # 100.5

    def test_sticky_mode_persists_until_stop(self):
        h = HumanInput(sticky=True)
        h.press(-1)
        with mock.patch("game.tui.time.monotonic", return_value=1e9):
            self.assertEqual(h(None, 0), -1)
        h.press(1)
        self.assertEqual(h(None, 0), 1)
        h.stop()
        self.assertEqual(h(None, 0), 0)


class TextTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_help_text(self):
        g, a = Glyphs(True), Glyphs(False)
        self.assertIn("W/S", key_help(0, False, False, False, g))
        self.assertIn("A/D", key_help(3, True, False, False, g))
        self.assertIn("steer", key_help(0, True, False, False, g))
        self.assertIn("bot", key_help(0, False, True, False, g))
        self.assertIn("out", key_help(0, False, False, True, g))
        self.assertIn("Up/Down", key_help(0, False, False, False, a))
        self.assertTrue(key_help(0, False, False, False, a).isascii())

    def test_ascii_glyphs_are_ascii(self):
        g = Glyphs(False)
        for name, value in vars(g).items():
            self.assertTrue(value.isascii(), name)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

=== FILE: test_report.py ===
import csv
import math
import os
import tempfile
import unittest

from analysis import report as R


class ReportTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def synthetic_summary(self, rows):
        d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        p = os.path.join(d, "summary.csv")
        header = ["mode", "bg_label", "bg_mbps", "players", "repeat",
                  "warmup", "tail", "duration",
                  "delay_mean_ms", "delay_p95_ms", "jitter_ms", "loss_pct",
                  "rtt_mean_ms", "rtt_p95_ms",
                  "server_delay_mean_ms", "server_delay_p95_ms", "server_jitter_ms", "server_loss_pct",
                  "folder"]
        with open(p, "w", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(header)
            for r in rows:
                w.writerow(r)
        return p

    def test_mean_std_on_identical_values(self):
        m, s = R.mean_std([10.0, 10.0, 10.0])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(m, 10.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s, 0.0, places=6)

    def test_mean_std_single_value(self):
        m, s = R.mean_std([5.0])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(m, 5.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s, 0.0, places=6)

    def test_aggregate_groups(self):
        p = self.synthetic_summary([
            ["baseline", "none", 0.0, 2, 1, 2.0, 1.0, 15, 10.0, 12.0, 1.0, 0.0, 5.0, 6.0, 8.0, 10.0, 0.5, 0.0, "f1"],
            ["baseline", "none", 0.0, 2, 2, 2.0, 1.0, 15, 14.0, 16.0, 3.0, 0.0, 7.0, 8.0, 10.0, 12.0, 0.7, 0.0, "f2"],
        ])
        agg = R.aggregate(p)
        # baseline/none/2/delay_mean -> mean 12, std 2
        d = [r for r in agg if r["mode"] == "baseline" and r["metric"] == "delay_mean_ms"][0]
        self.assertAlmostEqual(d["mean"], 12.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(d["std"], 2.0, places=6)

    def test_improvement_factor(self):
        self.assertAlmostEqual(R.improvement_factor(100.0, 20.0), 5.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(R.improvement_factor(0.0, 10.0), 0.0, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(R.improvement_factor(10.0, 0.0), 0.0, places=6)

    def test_group_by_key(self):
        rows = [
            {"mode": "baseline", "bg_label": "none", "players": "2", "bg_mbps": "0.0"},
            {"mode": "priority", "bg_label": "none", "players": "2", "bg_mbps": "0.0"},
        ]
        groups = R.group_by_key(rows)
        self.assertEqual(len(groups), 2)
        self.assertIn(("baseline", "none", 2, 0.0), groups)
        self.assertIn(("priority", "none", 2, 0.0), groups)

    def test_generate_markdown(self):
        p = self.synthetic_summary([
            ["baseline", "none", 0.0, 2, 1, 2.0, 1.0, 15, 10.0, 12.0, 1.0, 0.0, 5.0, 6.0, 8.0, 10.0, 0.5, 0.0, "f1"],
            ["priority", "none", 0.0, 2, 1, 2.0, 1.0, 15, 2.0, 3.0, 0.2, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 1.5, 2.5, 0.1, 0.0, "f2"],
        ])
        agg_rows = R.aggregate(p)
        d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        agg_path = os.path.join(d, "aggregate.csv")
        md_path = os.path.join(d, "analysis.md")
        with open(agg_path, "w", newline="") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(["mode", "bg_label", "players", "bg_mbps", "metric", "mean", "std", "n"])
            for r in agg_rows:
                w.writerow([r["mode"], r["bg_label"], r["players"], r["bg_mbps"],
                            r["metric"], r["mean"], r["std"], r["n"]])
        R.generate_markdown(agg_rows, md_path, p)
        with open(md_path, "r") as f:
            content = f.read()
        self.assertIn("Aggregate", content)
        self.assertIn("Baseline vs Priority", content)
        self.assertIn("5.00x", content)  # baseline 10 / priority 2 = 5

    def test_main_writes_files(self):
        p = self.synthetic_summary([
            ["baseline", "none", 0.0, 2, 1, 2.0, 1.0, 15, 10.0, 12.0, 1.0, 0.0, 5.0, 6.0, 8.0, 10.0, 0.5, 0.0, "f1"],
            ["priority", "none", 0.0, 2, 1, 2.0, 1.0, 15, 2.0, 3.0, 0.2, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 1.5, 2.5, 0.1, 0.0, "f2"],
        ])
        d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        out = os.path.join(d, "aggregate.csv")
        md = os.path.join(d, "analysis.md")
        rc = R.main([p, "--out", out, "--md", md])
        self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(out))
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(md))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

=== FOLDER: /tests ===
=== FILE: test_state.py ===
import math
import random
import unittest

from game import config as C
from game.protocol import PHASE_GAME_OVER, PHASE_PLAYING, PHASE_WAITING
from game.state import GameState

DT = 1.0 / C.TICK_HZ


def playing_state(players=(0, 1), seed=1):
    """A state that is already PLAYING with the serve delay used up."""
    s = GameState(seed=seed)
    for slot in players:
        s.set_active(slot, True)
    s.step(DT)                      # WAITING -> PLAYING
    assert s.phase == PHASE_PLAYING
    s.serve_timer = 0.0
    return s


class StateTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_waits_for_two_players(self):
        s = GameState(seed=1)
        s.set_active(0, True)
        for _ in range(100):
            s.step(DT)
        self.assertEqual(s.phase, PHASE_WAITING)
        self.assertEqual((s.ball_x, s.ball_y), (0.5, 0.5))

    def test_starts_with_two_players_and_freezes_ball_during_serve(self):
        s = GameState(seed=1)
        s.set_active(0, True)
        s.set_active(1, True)
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertEqual(s.phase, PHASE_PLAYING)
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertEqual((s.ball_x, s.ball_y), (0.5, 0.5))   # still frozen
        for _ in range(int(C.SERVE_DELAY / DT) + 3):
            s.step(DT)
        self.assertNotEqual((s.ball_x, s.ball_y), (0.5, 0.5))  # now moving

    def test_paddle_is_clamped_to_wall(self):
        s = GameState(seed=1)
        s.set_active(0, True)
        s.set_input(0, -1)
        for _ in range(200):
            s.step(DT)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s.paddles[0], C.PADDLE_LEN / 2)
        s.set_input(0, 1)
        for _ in range(400):
            s.step(DT)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(s.paddles[0], 1 - C.PADDLE_LEN / 2)

    def test_paddle_hit_reflects_without_losing_a_life(self):
        s = playing_state()
        s.ball_x, s.ball_y = 0.03, 0.5
        s.vx, s.vy = -0.4, 0.0
        s.paddles[0] = 0.5
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertGreater(s.vx, 0)
        self.assertEqual(s.lives[0], C.LIVES)

    def test_miss_costs_a_life_and_reserves(self):
        s = playing_state()
        s.ball_x, s.ball_y = 0.03, 0.9
        s.vx, s.vy = -0.4, 0.0
        s.paddles[0] = 0.1
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertEqual(s.lives[0], C.LIVES - 1)
        self.assertEqual((s.ball_x, s.ball_y), (0.5, 0.5))
        self.assertGreater(s.serve_timer, 0)

    def test_empty_slot_wall_is_solid(self):
        s = playing_state(players=(0, 1))
        s.ball_x, s.ball_y = 0.5, 0.03
        s.vx, s.vy = 0.0, -0.4          # heading for the top wall, slot 2 is empty
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertGreater(s.vy, 0)
        self.assertEqual(s.lives, [C.LIVES] * 4)

    def test_speed_is_preserved_after_a_bounce(self):
        s = playing_state()
        s.ball_x, s.ball_y = 0.03, 0.55
        s.vx, s.vy = -0.4, 0.0
        s.paddles[0] = 0.5
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(math.hypot(s.vx, s.vy), s.speed, places=6)

    def test_game_over_declares_winner_then_restarts(self):
        s = playing_state(players=(0, 1, 2))
        s.lives[0] = 0
        s.lives[2] = 0
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertEqual(s.phase, PHASE_GAME_OVER)
        self.assertEqual(s.winner, 1)
        for _ in range(int(C.GAME_OVER_DELAY / DT) + 3):
            s.step(DT)
        self.assertEqual(s.phase, PHASE_PLAYING)
        self.assertEqual([s.lives[i] for i in (0, 1, 2)], [C.LIVES] * 3)
        self.assertIsNone(s.winner)

    def test_player_leaving_ends_a_two_player_game(self):
        s = playing_state(players=(0, 1))
        s.set_active(1, False)
        s.step(DT)
        self.assertEqual(s.phase, PHASE_GAME_OVER)
        self.assertEqual(s.winner, 0)

    def test_same_seed_same_game(self):
        def run():
            s = GameState(seed=42)
            for slot in range(4):
                s.set_active(slot, True)
            rng = random.Random(7)
            for _ in range(900):
                for slot in range(4):
                    s.set_input(slot, rng.choice((-1, 0, 1)))
                s.step(DT)
            return s.snapshot()
        self.assertEqual(run(), run())

    def test_long_random_game_keeps_invariants(self):
        s = GameState(seed=3)
        for slot in range(4):
            s.set_active(slot, True)
        rng = random.Random(5)
        games_started = 0
        for _ in range(20000):
            for slot in range(4):
                s.set_input(slot, rng.choice((-1, 0, 1)))
            s.step(DT)
            if s.phase == PHASE_PLAYING and s.serve_timer > C.SERVE_DELAY - DT:
                games_started += 1
            self.assertTrue(-0.1 <= s.ball_x <= 1.1 and -0.1 <= s.ball_y <= 1.1)
            self.assertTrue(all(0 <= l <= C.LIVES for l in s.lives))
            self.assertTrue(all(C.PADDLE_LEN / 2 - 1e-9 <= p <= 1 - C.PADDLE_LEN / 2 + 1e-9
                                for p in s.paddles))
        self.assertGreater(games_started, 1)   # games end and restart on their own


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import unittest

from topo import scenarios as S


class ScenarioMatrixTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_matrix_dimensions(self):
        m = S.matrix(repeats=3)
        self.assertEqual(len(m), 2 * 3 * 3 * 3)  # 54 scenarios

    def test_quick_matrix(self):
        qm = S.quick_matrix()
        self.assertEqual(len(qm), 2)
        modes = [s.mode for s in qm]
        self.assertEqual(modes, ["baseline", "priority"])

    def test_folder_naming(self):
        s = S.Scenario(mode="baseline", bg_label="heavy", bg_mbps=20.0, players=4, repeat=2)
        self.assertEqual(s.folder_name, "baseline_heavy_4p_r2")
        self.assertEqual(S.folder_for(s), "results/raw/baseline_heavy_4p_r2")

    def test_scenario_properties(self):
        s_base = S.Scenario("baseline", "none", 0.0, 2, 1)
        self.assertEqual(s_base.qos, "plain")
        self.assertEqual(s_base.controller, "none")
        self.assertEqual(s_base.controller_file, "sdn/baseline_switch.py")

        s_prio = S.Scenario("priority", "heavy", 20.0, 4, 1)
        self.assertEqual(s_prio.qos, "priority")
        self.assertEqual(s_prio.controller, "remote")
        self.assertEqual(s_prio.controller_file, "sdn/qos_switch.py")

    def test_custom_repeats(self):
        m1 = S.matrix(repeats=1)
        self.assertEqual(len(m1), 2 * 3 * 3 * 1)  # 18 scenarios
        m2 = S.matrix(repeats=2)
        self.assertEqual(len(m2), 2 * 3 * 3 * 2)  # 36 scenarios


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

=== FOLDER: /tests ===
=== FILE: test_menu.py ===
import curses
import unittest

from game import menu
from game.menu import Settings


class FakeScreen:
    """A curses-like screen that clips like curses does, so the pure draw code can be tested."""

    def __init__(self, rows, cols):
        self.rows, self.cols = rows, cols
        self.buf = [[" "] * cols for _ in range(rows)]

    def getmaxyx(self):
        return self.rows, self.cols

    def addstr(self, y, x, text, attr=0):
        if y < 0 or y >= self.rows or x >= self.cols or not text:
            return
        if x < 0:
            text, x = text[-x:], 0
        for i, ch in enumerate(text[: self.cols - x]):
            self.buf[y][x + i] = ch

    def erase(self):
        self.buf = [[" "] * self.cols for _ in range(self.rows)]

    def refresh(self):
        pass

    def text(self):
        return "\n".join("".join(row) for row in self.buf)


def colors():
    c = menu.Colors()
    c.enabled = False       # no curses calls; attr() just returns the bold/dim bits
    return c


class EditTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_valid_host(self):
        st = Settings()
        self.assertTrue(menu.commit_edit(st, "host", "  10.0.0.1 "))
        self.assertEqual(st.host, "10.0.0.1")

    def test_invalid_hosts(self):
        st = Settings()
        self.assertFalse(menu.commit_edit(st, "host", ""))
        self.assertFalse(menu.commit_edit(st, "host", "bad host"))
        self.assertEqual(st.host, "127.0.0.1")

    def test_valid_port(self):
        st = Settings()
        for text, value in (("1", 1), ("5000", 5000), ("65535", 65535)):
            self.assertTrue(menu.commit_edit(st, "port", text))
            self.assertEqual(st.port, value)

    def test_invalid_ports(self):
        st = Settings()
        for text in ("", "0", "65536", "99999", "abcd", "50 0", "50.5"):
            self.assertFalse(menu.commit_edit(st, "port", text), text)
        self.assertEqual(st.port, menu.C.GAME_PORT)


class CycleTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_default_is_hold(self):
        self.assertFalse(Settings().sticky)

    def test_controls_toggles(self):
        st = Settings(sticky=True)
        menu.cycle(st, "controls")
        self.assertFalse(st.sticky)
        menu.cycle(st, "controls")
        self.assertTrue(st.sticky)

    def test_skill_wraps_both_ways(self):
        st = Settings(skill=0)
        menu.cycle(st, "skill", -1)
        self.assertEqual(st.skill, len(menu.SKILLS) - 1)
        menu.cycle(st, "skill", 1)
        self.assertEqual(st.skill, 0)

    def test_graphics_wraps(self):
        st = Settings(graphics=len(menu.GRAPHICS) - 1)
        menu.cycle(st, "graphics", 1)
        self.assertEqual(st.graphics, 0)

    def test_field_value_matches_settings(self):
        st = Settings(sticky=False, skill=2, graphics=1)
        self.assertEqual(menu.field_value(st, "controls"), "hold")
        self.assertEqual(menu.field_value(st, "skill"), menu.SKILLS[2][0])
        self.assertEqual(menu.field_value(st, "graphics"), menu.GRAPHICS[1][0])
        self.assertEqual(menu.field_value(st, "port"), str(st.port))
        self.assertEqual(menu.field_value(st, "back"), "")


class WrapTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_wrap_never_exceeds_width(self):
        for line in menu.wrap(menu.HELP["controls"], 20):
            self.assertLessEqual(len(line), 20)


class DrawTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_menu_fits_at_minimum(self):
        scr = FakeScreen(menu.MENU_MIN_ROWS, menu.MENU_MIN_COLS)
        menu.draw_menu(scr, colors(), Settings(), "")
        text = scr.text()
        for _, label in menu.ITEMS:
            self.assertIn(label, text, label)

    def test_menu_too_small_shows_message(self):
        scr = FakeScreen(rows=menu.MENU_MIN_ROWS - 1, cols=menu.MENU_MIN_COLS - 1)
        menu.draw_menu(scr, colors(), Settings(), "")
        self.assertIn("too small", scr.text())

    def test_settings_fits_at_minimum(self):
        scr = FakeScreen(menu.SETTINGS_MIN_ROWS, menu.SETTINGS_MIN_COLS)
        menu.draw_settings(scr, colors(), Settings(), 0, None, False, False)
        self.assertIn("SETTINGS", scr.text())

    def test_settings_too_small_shows_message(self):
        scr = FakeScreen(rows=menu.SETTINGS_MIN_ROWS - 1, cols=menu.SETTINGS_MIN_COLS - 1)
        menu.draw_settings(scr, colors(), Settings(), 0, None, False, False)
        self.assertIn("too small", scr.text())

    def test_menu_message_is_shown(self):
        scr = FakeScreen(30, 60)
        menu.draw_menu(scr, colors(), Settings(), "server is full")
        self.assertIn("server is full", scr.text())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

=== FOLDER: /tests ===
=== FILE: test_bot.py ===
import unittest
from game.bot import Bot, DEADBAND, REROLL_TICKS
from game.protocol import PHASE_PLAYING, Snapshot


def make_snapshot(phase=PHASE_PLAYING, tick=0, ball_x=0.5, ball_y=0.5,
                  paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5), lives=(3, 3, 3, 3),
                  active=(1, 1, 1, 1)):
    return Snapshot(tick=tick, phase=phase, ball_x=ball_x, ball_y=ball_y,
                    paddles=paddles, lives=lives, active=active)


class BotTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_perfect_bot_never_misses(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=42, error=0.0)
        snap = make_snapshot(ball_x=0.8, paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
        result = bot(snap, 2)  # slot 2 = bottom wall, uses ball_x
        self.assertEqual(result, 1)

    def test_bot_stays_still_when_lined_up(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=42, error=0.0)
        snap = make_snapshot(ball_y=0.5, paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
        result = bot(snap, 0)  # slot 0 = left wall, uses ball_y
        self.assertEqual(result, 0)

    def test_bot_moves_away_when_ball_far(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=42, error=0.0)
        snap = make_snapshot(ball_y=0.9, paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
        result = bot(snap, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result, 1)

    def test_bot_moves_negative_when_ball_behind(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=42, error=0.0)
        snap = make_snapshot(ball_y=0.1, paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
        result = bot(snap, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result, -1)

    def test_bot_returns_zero_when_not_playing(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=42, error=0.0)
        snap = make_snapshot(phase=0)  # not playing
        result = bot(snap, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result, 0)

    def test_bot_returns_zero_when_eliminated(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=42, error=0.0)
        snap = make_snapshot(lives=(0, 3, 3, 3))  # slot 0 dead
        result = bot(snap, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result, 0)

    def test_bot_returns_zero_when_inactive(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=42, error=0.0)
        snap = make_snapshot(active=(0, 1, 1, 1))  # slot 0 inactive
        result = bot(snap, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result, 0)

    def test_bot_seed_reproducibility(self):
        bot1 = Bot(seed=123)
        bot2 = Bot(seed=123)
        snap = make_snapshot(tick=100, ball_y=0.7, paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
        self.assertEqual(bot1(snap, 0), bot2(snap, 0))

    def test_bot_error_causes_movement_variance(self):
        bot = Bot(seed=1, error=0.5)  # large error
        snap = make_snapshot(ball_y=0.5, paddles=(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
        result = bot(snap, 0)
        self.assertIn(result, (-1, 0, 1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: signals.py ===
"""Make SIGTERM behave like Ctrl-C so processes started by the experiment scripts
can be stopped cleanly (log files flushed, LEAVE sent)."""
import signal


def exit_on_signals() -> None:
    def handler(signum, frame):
        raise KeyboardInterrupt

    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, handler)
    # Also re-enables Ctrl-C if this process inherited "ignore SIGINT"
    # (which happens for background jobs started by a non-interactive shell).
    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, handler)

=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: server.py ===
"""UDP game server.

Two threads, as in the design:
  * receive thread  - parses packets, registers/removes players, stores each
                      player's latest input (only if its seq is newer)
  * tick thread     - every 1/TICK_HZ s: evict silent players, advance the
                      simulation, broadcast the whole world to every player

Run:  python3 -m game.server --host 0.0.0.0 --port 5000 --log results/raw/server.csv
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import logging
import os
import random
import socket
import threading
import time
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from . import config as C
from . import protocol as P
from .seq import NEW, SeqTracker
from .signals import exit_on_signals
from .state import GameState

log = logging.getLogger("server")
Addr = Tuple[str, int]


class Player:
    def __init__(self, slot: int, addr: Addr, t: float) -> None:
        self.slot = slot
        self.addr = addr
        self.last_seen = t
        self.tracker = SeqTracker()   # this player's STATE_UPDATE stream


class GameServer:
    def __init__(self, host: str = "0.0.0.0", port: int = C.GAME_PORT,
                 log_path: Optional[str] = None, drop_prob: float = 0.0,
                 seed: Optional[int] = None, tick_hz: int = C.TICK_HZ,
                 player_timeout: float = C.PLAYER_TIMEOUT,
                 loss_seed: Optional[int] = None) -> None:
        self.sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        self.sock.bind((host, port))
        self.sock.settimeout(0.2)
        self.port = self.sock.getsockname()[1]
        self.tick_hz = tick_hz
        self.player_timeout = player_timeout
        self.drop_prob = drop_prob
        self._loss_rng = random.Random(loss_seed)

        self.state = GameState(seed=seed)
        self.players: Dict[Addr, Player] = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.bad_packets = 0
        self.dropped = 0
        self._bcast_seq = 0
        self._pong_seq = 0
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._threads: List[threading.Thread] = []

        self._log_file = None
        self._log_csv = None
        if log_path:
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(log_path)), exist_ok=True)
            self._log_file = open(log_path, "w", newline="")
            self._log_csv = csv.writer(self._log_file)
            self._log_csv.writerow(["recv_time", "player", "seq", "send_time", "kind"])

    # --- lifecycle -----------------------------------------------------------

    def start(self) -> None:
        for fn, name in ((self._recv_loop, "recv"), (self._tick_loop, "tick")):
            t = threading.Thread(target=fn, name="server-" + name, daemon=True)
            t.start()
            self._threads.append(t)
        log.info("listening on UDP %d", self.port)

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        for t in self._threads:
            t.join(timeout=2)
        self.sock.close()
        if self._log_file:
            self._log_file.close()
            self._log_file = None

    # --- hooks for the SDN controller integration (deliverable 2) ------------

    def on_join(self, player: Player) -> None:
        """Called (lock held) when a player registers.  D2: tell the controller."""

    def on_leave(self, player: Player, reason: str) -> None:
        """Called (lock held) when a player leaves or times out.  D2: tell the controller."""

    # --- receive side ----------------------------------------------------------

    def _recv_loop(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            try:
                data, addr = self.sock.recvfrom(2048)
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except OSError:
                break
            if self.drop_prob and self._loss_rng.random() < self.drop_prob:
                self.dropped += 1
                continue
            t_recv = P.now()
            try:
                hdr, payload = P.decode(data)
            except P.ProtocolError:
                self.bad_packets += 1
                continue
            with self.lock:
                self._handle(hdr, payload, addr, t_recv)

    def _handle(self, hdr: P.Header, payload: bytes, addr: Addr, t_recv: float) -> None:
        player = self.players.get(addr)
        if player:
            player.last_seen = t_recv

        if hdr.msg_type == P.JOIN:
            if player is None:
                slot = next((i for i in range(C.MAX_PLAYERS)
                             if all(p.slot != i for p in self.players.values())), None)
                if slot is None:
                    log.info("rejected %s: server full", addr)
                    self._send(addr, P.encode(P.JOIN_ACK, 0, C.NO_PLAYER))
                    return
                player = Player(slot, addr, t_recv)
                self.players[addr] = player
                self.state.set_active(slot, True)
                log.info("player %d joined from %s", slot, addr)
                self.on_join(player)
            self._send(addr, P.encode(P.JOIN_ACK, 0, player.slot))   # idempotent: also answers retries

        elif hdr.msg_type == P.STATE_UPDATE and player:
            kind, _gap = player.tracker.observe(hdr.seq)
            if kind == NEW:                       # old/duplicate/late updates are ignored
                self.state.set_input(player.slot, P.unpack_update(payload))
            if self._log_csv:
                self._log_csv.writerow(["%.6f" % t_recv, player.slot, hdr.seq,
                                        "%.6f" % hdr.send_time, kind])

        elif hdr.msg_type == P.PING:
            self._send(addr, P.encode(P.PONG, self._pong_seq, player.slot if player else 0,
                                      P.pack_echo(hdr.send_time)))
            self._pong_seq += 1

        elif hdr.msg_type == P.LEAVE and player:
            self._remove(player, "left")

    def _remove(self, player: Player, reason: str) -> None:
        self.players.pop(player.addr, None)
        self.state.set_active(player.slot, False)
        log.info("player %d %s", player.slot, reason)
        self.on_leave(player, reason)

    # --- tick side ---------------------------------------------------------------

    def _tick_loop(self) -> None:
        dt = 1.0 / self.tick_hz
        next_t = time.monotonic()
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            next_t += dt
            with self.lock:
                t = P.now()
                for p in [p for p in self.players.values() if t - p.last_seen > self.player_timeout]:
                    self._remove(p, "timed out")
                self.state.step(dt)
                seq = self._bcast_seq
                self._bcast_seq += 1
                pkt = P.encode(P.STATE_BROADCAST, seq, 0, P.pack_broadcast(self.state.snapshot()))
                addrs = list(self.players)
            for addr in addrs:
                self._send(addr, pkt)
            delay = next_t - time.monotonic()
            if delay > 0:
                self._stop.wait(delay)
            elif delay < -dt:                    # fell badly behind: resync, don't burst
                next_t = time.monotonic()

    def _send(self, addr: Addr, data: bytes) -> None:
        try:
            self.sock.sendto(data, addr)
        except OSError:
            pass

    # --- reporting ---------------------------------------------------------------

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        with self.lock:
            return {
                "tick": self.state.tick,
                "phase": self.state.phase,
                "players": len(self.players),
                "bad_packets": self.bad_packets,
                "dropped": self.dropped,
                "update_loss": {p.slot: (p.tracker.received, p.tracker.lost)
                                for p in self.players.values()},
            }


def main(argv=None) -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python3 -m game.server", description="4-player pong UDP server")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="0.0.0.0")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=C.GAME_PORT)
    ap.add_argument("--log", help="CSV of every STATE_UPDATE received (for latency/loss analysis)")
    ap.add_argument("--drop", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("GAME_DROP_PROB", "0")),
                    help="testing aid: drop this fraction of received packets (or env GAME_DROP_PROB)")
    ap.add_argument("--stats-interval", type=float, default=5.0, help="seconds between status lines (0=off)")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    exit_on_signals()
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(name)s: %(message)s", datefmt="%H:%M:%S")
    server = GameServer(args.host, args.port, log_path=args.log, drop_prob=args.drop)
    server.start()
    try:
        while True:
            time.sleep(args.stats_interval or 3600)
            if args.stats_interval:
                log.info("stats %s", server.stats())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log.info("shutting down")
    finally:
        server.stop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: config.py ===
"""Shared constants.

Imported by the game, the Mininet scripts and (later) the Ryu controller, so the
UDP port and timing values live in exactly one place.  Keep this file free of
imports and of syntax newer than Python 3.9 (the Ryu environment is 3.9).
"""

# --- network ---------------------------------------------------------------
GAME_PORT = 5000          # the UDP port the SDN controller matches on
TICK_HZ = 30              # server simulation/broadcast rate AND client update rate
MAX_PLAYERS = 4
NO_PLAYER = 0xFF          # player_id in a JOIN_ACK that means "server full"

JOIN_RETRIES = 3          # JOIN is the only message that is retried
JOIN_TIMEOUT = 0.5        # seconds to wait for JOIN_ACK per attempt
PLAYER_TIMEOUT = 3.0      # server drops a player silent for this long
PING_INTERVAL = 1.0       # client PING period (RTT samples)

# --- SDN queues (OVS QoS queue ids on the bottleneck ports) -------------------
QUEUE_GAME = 1            # high priority: the game's UDP port
QUEUE_BG = 2              # everything else

# --- game ------------------------------------------------------------------
# The arena is the unit square [0, 1] x [0, 1].
LIVES = 3
PADDLE_LEN = 0.20         # fraction of the wall a paddle covers
PADDLE_SPEED = 0.90       # arena units per second
BALL_RADIUS = 0.02
BALL_SPEED = 0.45         # arena units per second at serve
BALL_SPEEDUP = 1.04       # multiplier on every paddle hit
BALL_MAX_SPEED = 0.90
SERVE_DELAY = 1.0         # ball is frozen this long after every serve
GAME_OVER_DELAY = 3.0     # pause before the next game starts automatically

=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: protocol.py ===
"""Wire format of the game (all UDP, all big-endian).

Every packet = 15-byte header + fixed-size payload:

    version  u8
    type     u8
    seq      u32     sequence number of this packet within its *stream*
    send_time f64    time.monotonic() of the sender when it built the packet
    player_id u8     slot 0..3 of the player involved (see per-message notes)

A *stream* is one sender + one message type, each with its own counter:
  client -> server STATE_UPDATE   (one stream per client)
  client -> server PING           (separate counter, so it never creates gaps in
                                   the STATE_UPDATE stream)
  server -> clients STATE_BROADCAST (one global counter; each client sees its own
                                   gaps = its own loss)
  server -> client PONG

``send_time`` uses the monotonic clock.  Every Mininet host runs on the same
kernel, so it is one shared clock and ``recv_time - send_time`` is a true
one-way delay.  (It would NOT be meaningful between two physical machines.)

Message payloads:
  JOIN             (empty)           seq 0, retried if no JOIN_ACK
  JOIN_ACK         (empty)           header.player_id = assigned slot, or NO_PLAYER if full
  STATE_UPDATE     i8 input          -1 / 0 / +1: the paddle direction held right now
  STATE_BROADCAST  see BROADCAST     the whole world, sent every tick
  PING             (empty)
  PONG             f64               the PING's send_time echoed back
  LEAVE            (empty)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import struct
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Tuple

VERSION = 1

JOIN = 1
JOIN_ACK = 2
STATE_UPDATE = 3
STATE_BROADCAST = 4
PING = 5
PONG = 6
LEAVE = 7

TYPE_NAMES = {
    JOIN: "JOIN",
    JOIN_ACK: "JOIN_ACK",
    STATE_UPDATE: "STATE_UPDATE",
    STATE_BROADCAST: "STATE_BROADCAST",
    PING: "PING",
    PONG: "PONG",
    LEAVE: "LEAVE",
}

PHASE_WAITING = 0    # fewer than 2 players
PHASE_PLAYING = 1
PHASE_GAME_OVER = 2

HEADER = struct.Struct("!BBIdB")
UPDATE = struct.Struct("!b")
ECHO = struct.Struct("!d")
# tick, phase, ball x/y, 4 paddle positions, 4 lives, 4 "slot occupied" flags
BROADCAST = struct.Struct("!IB2f4f4B4B")

PAYLOAD_SIZE = {
    JOIN: 0,
    JOIN_ACK: 0,
    STATE_UPDATE: UPDATE.size,
    STATE_BROADCAST: BROADCAST.size,
    PING: 0,
    PONG: ECHO.size,
    LEAVE: 0,
}

SEQ_MASK = 0xFFFFFFFF


class ProtocolError(ValueError):
    """Raised for packets that must be dropped (short, wrong version, bad size...)."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Header:
    version: int
    msg_type: int
    seq: int
    send_time: float
    player_id: int


@dataclass
class Snapshot:
    """The whole game world as sent in a STATE_BROADCAST."""
    tick: int
    phase: int
    ball_x: float
    ball_y: float
    paddles: Tuple[float, float, float, float]
    lives: Tuple[int, int, int, int]
    active: Tuple[int, int, int, int]


def now() -> float:
    return time.monotonic()


def encode(msg_type: int, seq: int, player_id: int = 0, payload: bytes = b"",
           send_time: float = None) -> bytes:
    if send_time is None:
        send_time = now()
    return HEADER.pack(VERSION, msg_type, seq & SEQ_MASK, send_time, player_id & 0xFF) + payload


def decode(data: bytes) -> Tuple[Header, bytes]:
    """Parse and validate a packet.  Raises ProtocolError if it must be dropped."""
    if len(data) < HEADER.size:
        raise ProtocolError("short packet (%d bytes)" % len(data))
    version, msg_type, seq, send_time, player_id = HEADER.unpack_from(data)
    if version != VERSION:
        raise ProtocolError("unsupported version %d" % version)
    if msg_type not in TYPE_NAMES:
        raise ProtocolError("unknown message type %d" % msg_type)
    payload = data[HEADER.size:]
    if len(payload) != PAYLOAD_SIZE[msg_type]:
        raise ProtocolError("%s payload is %d bytes, expected %d"
                            % (TYPE_NAMES[msg_type], len(payload), PAYLOAD_SIZE[msg_type]))
    return Header(version, msg_type, seq, send_time, player_id), payload


# --- payload helpers --------------------------------------------------------

def pack_update(direction: int) -> bytes:
    return UPDATE.pack(max(-1, min(1, int(direction))))


def unpack_update(payload: bytes) -> int:
    try:
        return max(-1, min(1, UPDATE.unpack(payload)[0]))
    except struct.error as exc:
        raise ProtocolError(str(exc))


def pack_echo(t: float) -> bytes:
    return ECHO.pack(t)


def unpack_echo(payload: bytes) -> float:
    try:
        return ECHO.unpack(payload)[0]
    except struct.error as exc:
        raise ProtocolError(str(exc))


def pack_broadcast(s: Snapshot) -> bytes:
    return BROADCAST.pack(s.tick & SEQ_MASK, s.phase, s.ball_x, s.ball_y,
                          *s.paddles, *s.lives, *s.active)


def unpack_broadcast(payload: bytes) -> Snapshot:
    try:
        v = BROADCAST.unpack(payload)
    except struct.error as exc:
        raise ProtocolError(str(exc))
    return Snapshot(tick=v[0], phase=v[1], ball_x=v[2], ball_y=v[3],
                    paddles=tuple(v[4:8]), lives=tuple(v[8:12]), active=tuple(v[12:16]))

=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: client.py ===
"""UDP game client: one player, human or bot, with or without the TUI.

Examples (run from the pong/ folder):
    python3 -m game.client --server 10.0.0.1                      # play yourself in the TUI
    python3 -m game.client --server 10.0.0.1 --bot                # watch a bot play in the TUI
    python3 -m game.client --server 10.0.0.1 --bot --headless \
            --duration 60 --log results/raw/h1.csv                # experiment run, no UI

The network part (GameClient) knows nothing about how the paddle is controlled:
it just calls `input_fn(snapshot, slot) -> -1/0/+1` every tick.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import logging
import os
import random
import socket
import sys
import threading
import time
from typing import Callable, Optional

from . import config as C
from . import protocol as P
from .seq import NEW, SeqTracker
from .signals import exit_on_signals

log = logging.getLogger("client")
InputFn = Callable[[Optional[P.Snapshot], int], int]


class JoinError(Exception):
    pass


class GameClient:
    def __init__(self, server: str, port: int = C.GAME_PORT, input_fn: Optional[InputFn] = None,
                 log_path: Optional[str] = None, drop_prob: float = 0.0,
                 tick_hz: int = C.TICK_HZ, join_retries: int = C.JOIN_RETRIES,
                 join_timeout: float = C.JOIN_TIMEOUT, loss_seed: Optional[int] = None) -> None:
        self.addr = (socket.gethostbyname(server), port)
        self.sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        self.sock.bind(("", 0))
        self.sock.settimeout(0.2)
        self.input_fn = input_fn
        self.tick_hz = tick_hz
        self.join_retries = join_retries
        self.join_timeout = join_timeout
        self.drop_prob = drop_prob
        self._loss_rng = random.Random(loss_seed)

        self.player_id: Optional[int] = None
        self.tracker = SeqTracker()            # the server's STATE_BROADCAST stream, as seen by me
        self.dropped = 0
        self.bad_packets = 0
        self.rtt: Optional[float] = None
        self._rtt_sum = 0.0
        self._rtt_n = 0

        self._snapshot: Optional[P.Snapshot] = None
        self._last_rx = 0.0
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._update_seq = 0
        self._ping_seq = 0
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._threads = []

        self._log_file = None
        self._log_csv = None
        if log_path:
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(log_path)), exist_ok=True)
            self._log_file = open(log_path, "w", newline="")
            self._log_csv = csv.writer(self._log_file)
            # kind = new/late/duplicate/stale for broadcasts; pong rows carry the ping's send_time
            self._log_csv.writerow(["kind", "recv_time", "seq", "send_time"])

    # --- joining -----------------------------------------------------------------

    def join(self) -> int:
        """JOIN, retrying if there is no reply.  Returns my player slot."""
        for attempt in range(1, self.join_retries + 1):
            self._send(P.encode(P.JOIN, 0, 0))
            deadline = time.monotonic() + self.join_timeout
            while True:
                remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if remaining <= 0:
                    break
                self.sock.settimeout(max(0.01, remaining))
                try:
                    data, addr = self.sock.recvfrom(2048)
                except socket.timeout:
                    break
                if addr != self.addr:
                    continue
                try:
                    hdr, _ = P.decode(data)
                except P.ProtocolError:
                    continue
                if hdr.msg_type == P.JOIN_ACK:
                    self.sock.settimeout(0.2)
                    if hdr.player_id == C.NO_PLAYER:
                        raise JoinError("server is full")
                    self.player_id = hdr.player_id
                    return self.player_id
            log.info("no JOIN_ACK (attempt %d/%d)", attempt, self.join_retries)
        self.sock.settimeout(0.2)
        raise JoinError("no reply from server %s:%d" % self.addr)

    # --- running ---------------------------------------------------------------

    def start(self) -> None:
        if self.player_id is None:
            raise RuntimeError("join() first")
        for fn, name in ((self._send_loop, "send"), (self._recv_loop, "recv")):
            t = threading.Thread(target=fn, name="client-" + name, daemon=True)
            t.start()
            self._threads.append(t)

    def leave(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        for t in self._threads:
            t.join(timeout=2)
        if self.player_id is not None:
            for _ in range(2):                        # one retry, LEAVE is cheap
                self._send(P.encode(P.LEAVE, 0, self.player_id))
        try:
            self.sock.close()
        except OSError:
            pass
        if self._log_file:
            self._log_file.close()
            self._log_file = None

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc):
        self.leave()

    def get_snapshot(self) -> Optional[P.Snapshot]:
        with self._lock:
            return self._snapshot

    def seconds_since_data(self) -> float:
        return time.monotonic() - self._last_rx if self._last_rx else float("inf")

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        t = self.tracker
        return {
            "expected": t.expected, "received": t.received, "lost": t.lost,
            "loss_ratio": t.loss_ratio, "late": t.late, "duplicates": t.duplicates,
            "rtt": self.rtt, "rtt_avg": self._rtt_sum / self._rtt_n if self._rtt_n else None,
        }

    # --- threads ---------------------------------------------------------------

    def _send_loop(self) -> None:
        dt = 1.0 / self.tick_hz
        next_t = time.monotonic()
        last_ping = 0.0
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            next_t += dt
            direction = 0
            if self.input_fn:
                try:
                    direction = self.input_fn(self.get_snapshot(), self.player_id)
                except Exception:                      # never let a UI bug kill the network thread
                    log.exception("input_fn failed")
            self._send(P.encode(P.STATE_UPDATE, self._update_seq, self.player_id, P.pack_update(direction)))
            self._update_seq += 1
            if time.monotonic() - last_ping >= C.PING_INTERVAL:
                last_ping = time.monotonic()
                self._send(P.encode(P.PING, self._ping_seq, self.player_id))
                self._ping_seq += 1
            delay = next_t - time.monotonic()
            if delay > 0:
                self._stop.wait(delay)
            elif delay < -dt:
                next_t = time.monotonic()

    def _recv_loop(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            try:
                data, addr = self.sock.recvfrom(2048)
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except OSError:
                break
            if addr != self.addr:
                continue
            if self.drop_prob and self._loss_rng.random() < self.drop_prob:
                self.dropped += 1
                continue
            t_recv = P.now()
            try:
                hdr, payload = P.decode(data)
                if hdr.msg_type == P.STATE_BROADCAST:
                    snap = P.unpack_broadcast(payload)
                    kind, _gap = self.tracker.observe(hdr.seq)
                    if self._log_csv:
                        self._log_csv.writerow([kind, "%.6f" % t_recv, hdr.seq, "%.6f" % hdr.send_time])
                    if kind == NEW:                    # only the newest state is ever shown
                        with self._lock:
                            self._snapshot = snap
                            self._last_rx = time.monotonic()
                elif hdr.msg_type == P.PONG:
                    echoed = P.unpack_echo(payload)
                    self.rtt = t_recv - echoed
                    self._rtt_sum += self.rtt
                    self._rtt_n += 1
                    if self._log_csv:
                        self._log_csv.writerow(["pong", "%.6f" % t_recv, hdr.seq, "%.6f" % echoed])
            except P.ProtocolError:
                self.bad_packets += 1

    def _send(self, data: bytes) -> None:
        try:
            self.sock.sendto(data, self.addr)
        except OSError:
            pass


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python3 -m game.client", description="4-player pong client")
    ap.add_argument("--server", default="127.0.0.1", help="server IP or name")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=C.GAME_PORT)
    ap.add_argument("--bot", action="store_true", help="let the computer control the paddle")
    ap.add_argument("--headless", action="store_true", help="no UI (bots only) - for experiments")
    ap.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=0, help="leave after N seconds (0 = until you quit)")
    ap.add_argument("--log", help="CSV of every broadcast received (for latency/jitter/loss analysis)")
    sticky_group = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    sticky_group.add_argument("--sticky", action="store_true",
                             help="keys set a direction that persists until you press space or the opposite key")
    sticky_group.add_argument("--hold", action="store_false", dest="sticky", default=False,
                             help="move only while the key repeats (default)")
    ap.add_argument("--ascii", action="store_true", help="plain ASCII graphics (for terminals without Unicode)")
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, help="bot random seed")
    ap.add_argument("--drop", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("GAME_DROP_PROB", "0")),
                    help="testing aid: drop this fraction of received packets (or env GAME_DROP_PROB)")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if args.headless and not args.bot:
        ap.error("--headless needs --bot (a human cannot play without the UI)")

    exit_on_signals()
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(name)s: %(message)s", datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
                        stream=sys.stderr)
    human = None
    if args.bot:
        from .bot import Bot
        input_fn: InputFn = Bot(seed=args.seed)
    else:
        from .tui import HumanInput
        human = HumanInput(sticky=args.sticky)
        input_fn = human

    client = GameClient(args.server, args.port, input_fn=input_fn, log_path=args.log, drop_prob=args.drop)
    try:
        slot = client.join()
    except JoinError as exc:
        print("could not join: %s" % exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    log.info("joined as player %d", slot)
    client.start()
    try:
        if args.headless:
            end = time.monotonic() + args.duration if args.duration else None
            while end is None or time.monotonic() < end:
                time.sleep(0.2)
        else:
            from .tui import run
            run(client, human, args.duration, ascii_mode=True if args.ascii else None)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        client.leave()
    s = client.stats()
    log.info("player %d done: received %d broadcasts, lost %d (%.2f%%), avg rtt %s",
             slot, s["received"], s["lost"], 100 * s["loss_ratio"],
             "%.2f ms" % (1000 * s["rtt_avg"]) if s["rtt_avg"] is not None else "n/a")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: __init__.py ===
from .menu import *
=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: menu.py ===
"""Title screen, menu and settings.   Run:  python3 -m game

    Play vs. bots     starts a private server on this machine plus three bots, and you play
    Join a server     connects to the server address in Settings (e.g. the Mininet server)
    Watch bots play   a local game with four bots; you just watch
    Settings          server address/port, sticky or hold controls, bot skill, graphics
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import curses
import locale
import os
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple

from . import config as C
from . import tui
from .bot import Bot
from .client import GameClient, JoinError
from .server import GameServer
from .signals import exit_on_signals
from .tui import (ACCENT, BAD, DIM, P1, TEXT, WARN, Colors, Glyphs, HumanInput, put)

MENU_MIN_ROWS, MENU_MIN_COLS = 13, 38          # smallest window the menu fits in
SETTINGS_MIN_ROWS, SETTINGS_MIN_COLS = 17, 52  # ... and the settings screen

# the lower the aiming error, the harder the bot
SKILLS = (("easy", 0.32), ("normal", 0.14), ("hard", 0.03))
GRAPHICS = (("auto", None), ("unicode", False), ("ascii", True))

# a 5x5 block font for the wordmark (original; only the letters we need)
FONT = {
    "P": ("####.", "#...#", "####.", "#....", "#...."),
    "O": (".###.", "#...#", "#...#", "#...#", ".###."),
    "N": ("#...#", "##..#", "#.#.#", "#..##", "#...#"),
    "G": (".####", "#....", "#..##", "#...#", ".###."),
}

ITEMS = (("local", "Play vs. bots"),
         ("join", "Join a server"),
         ("watch", "Watch bots play"),
         ("settings", "Settings"),
         ("exit", "Exit"))


@dataclass
class Settings:
    host: str = "127.0.0.1"
    port: int = C.GAME_PORT
    sticky: bool = False
    skill: int = 1                   # index into SKILLS
    graphics: int = 0                # index into GRAPHICS
    selected: int = 0                # last menu row

    @property
    def ascii_mode(self) -> Optional[bool]:
        return GRAPHICS[self.graphics][1]

    @property
    def bot_error(self) -> float:
        return SKILLS[self.skill][1]

    @property
    def unicode_ok(self) -> bool:
        return tui.want_unicode() if self.ascii_mode is None else not self.ascii_mode


# =============================================================================
# drawing
# =============================================================================

def logo_lines(unicode_ok: bool) -> List[List[Tuple[str, int]]]:
    """The wordmark as 5 rows of (text, colour) pieces; each letter has its player's colour."""
    on = "██" if unicode_ok else "##"
    rows = []
    for r in range(5):
        pieces = []
        for i, letter in enumerate("PONG"):
            pieces.append(("".join(on if c == "#" else "  " for c in FONT[letter][r]), P1 + i))
            if i < 3:
                pieces.append(("  ", DIM))
        rows.append(pieces)
    return rows


def box(scr, colors: Colors, g: Glyphs, y: int, x: int, h: int, w: int, color: int = ACCENT,
        title: str = "") -> None:
    """A rounded box with a clear interior (so it can sit on top of other drawing)."""
    attr = colors.attr(color)
    top = g.b_tl + g.b_h * (w - 2) + g.b_tr
    if title:
        t = " %s " % title
        top = g.b_tl + g.b_h * 2 + t + g.b_h * max(0, w - 4 - len(t)) + g.b_tr
    put(scr, y, x, top[:w], attr)
    for r in range(1, h - 1):
        put(scr, y + r, x, g.b_v + " " * (w - 2) + g.b_v, attr)
    put(scr, y + h - 1, x, g.b_bl + g.b_h * (w - 2) + g.b_br, attr)


def centered(scr, y: int, text: str, attr: int) -> None:
    _, cols = scr.getmaxyx()
    put(scr, y, max(0, (cols - len(text)) // 2), text, attr)


def too_small(scr, colors: Colors, need_cols: int, need_rows: int) -> None:
    rows, _ = scr.getmaxyx()
    centered(scr, rows // 2, "Window too small (need %dx%d)" % (need_cols, need_rows),
             colors.attr(BAD, bold=True))
    scr.refresh()


def draw_menu(scr, colors: Colors, st: Settings, message: str) -> None:
    g = Glyphs(st.unicode_ok)
    scr.erase()
    rows, cols = scr.getmaxyx()
    if rows < MENU_MIN_ROWS or cols < MENU_MIN_COLS:
        too_small(scr, colors, MENU_MIN_COLS, MENU_MIN_ROWS)
        return
    box_w = 34
    box_h = len(ITEMS) + 4
    logo = logo_lines(st.unicode_ok)
    big = rows >= box_h + 15 and cols >= 50          # logo + subtitles
    medium = rows >= box_h + 12 and cols >= 50       # logo only
    head_h = 8 if big else (6 if medium else 2)
    total = head_h + box_h + 4
    y = max(0, (rows - total) // 2)

    if big or medium:
        if big:
            centered(scr, y, "4 - P L A Y E R", colors.attr(DIM))
            y += 1
        width = sum(len(t) for t, _ in logo[0])
        for r, pieces in enumerate(logo):
            x = max(0, (cols - width) // 2)
            for text, col in pieces:
                put(scr, y + r, x, text, colors.attr(col, bold=True))
                x += len(text)
        y += 5
        if big:
            y += 1
            centered(scr, y, "UDP  %s  SDN  %s  Mininet" % (g.sep, g.sep), colors.attr(DIM))
            y += 1
        y += 1
    else:
        title = "4-PLAYER PONG"
        x = max(0, (cols - len(title)) // 2)
        for i, ch in enumerate(title):
            put(scr, y, x + i, ch, colors.attr(P1 + i % 4, bold=True))
        y += 2

    x0 = max(0, (cols - box_w) // 2)
    box(scr, colors, g, y, x0, box_h, box_w)
    for i, (_, label) in enumerate(ITEMS):
        row = y + 2 + i
        if i == st.selected:
            text = "%s %s %s" % (g.mark_l, label, g.mark_r)
            put(scr, row, x0 + (box_w - len(text)) // 2, text, colors.attr(P1, bold=True))
        else:
            put(scr, row, x0 + (box_w - len(label)) // 2, label, colors.attr(TEXT))
    y += box_h + 1

    if message:
        centered(scr, y, message[:cols - 2], colors.attr(BAD, bold=True))
    elif ITEMS[st.selected][0] == "join":
        centered(scr, y, "server %s:%d" % (st.host, st.port), colors.attr(DIM))
    y += 1
    centered(scr, y, "%s/%s select  %s  Enter choose  %s  Q quit" % (g.up, g.down, g.sep, g.sep), colors.attr(DIM))
    scr.refresh()


# =============================================================================
# settings screen
# =============================================================================

FIELDS = ("host", "port", "controls", "skill", "graphics", "back")
LABELS = {"host": "Server address", "port": "Server port", "controls": "Controls",
          "skill": "Bot skill", "graphics": "Graphics", "back": "Back"}
HELP = {"host": "Where 'Join a server' connects. Inside Mininet the game server is 10.0.0.10.",
        "port": "UDP port of the game server (the SDN controller matches on %d)." % C.GAME_PORT,
        "controls": "sticky: a key sets a direction until Space or the opposite key. hold: move only while keys repeat.",
        "skill": "How well the bots aim: easy, normal or hard.",
        "graphics": "auto picks Unicode when the terminal supports it; ascii is for old terminals.",
        "back": ""}


def field_value(st: Settings, name: str) -> str:
    if name == "host":
        return st.host
    if name == "port":
        return str(st.port)
    if name == "controls":
        return "sticky" if st.sticky else "hold"
    if name == "skill":
        return SKILLS[st.skill][0]
    if name == "graphics":
        return GRAPHICS[st.graphics][0]
    return ""


def cycle(st: Settings, name: str, step: int = 1) -> None:
    if name == "controls":
        st.sticky = not st.sticky
    elif name == "skill":
        st.skill = (st.skill + step) % len(SKILLS)
    elif name == "graphics":
        st.graphics = (st.graphics + step) % len(GRAPHICS)


def commit_edit(st: Settings, name: str, text: str) -> bool:
    text = text.strip()
    if name == "host":
        if text and " " not in text:
            st.host = text
            return True
    elif name == "port":
        if text.isascii() and text.isdigit() and 1 <= int(text) <= 65535:
            st.port = int(text)
            return True
    return False


def draw_settings(scr, colors: Colors, st: Settings, sel: int, editing: Optional[str], bad_input: bool,
                  fresh: bool = False) -> None:
    g = Glyphs(st.unicode_ok)
    scr.erase()
    rows, cols = scr.getmaxyx()
    if rows < SETTINGS_MIN_ROWS or cols < SETTINGS_MIN_COLS:
        too_small(scr, colors, SETTINGS_MIN_COLS, SETTINGS_MIN_ROWS)
        return
    w = 50
    h = len(FIELDS) + 5
    x0 = max(0, (cols - w) // 2)
    y0 = max(0, (rows - h - 4) // 2)
    box(scr, colors, g, y0, x0, h, w, title="SETTINGS")
    for i, name in enumerate(FIELDS):
        y = y0 + 2 + i + (1 if name == "back" else 0)
        chosen = i == sel
        label = LABELS[name]
        if name == "back":
            text = "%s %s" % (g.mark_l if chosen else " ", label)
            put(scr, y, x0 + 3, text, colors.attr(P1 if chosen else TEXT, bold=chosen))
            continue
        put(scr, y, x0 + 3, g.mark_l if chosen else " ", colors.attr(P1, bold=True))
        put(scr, y, x0 + 5, label, colors.attr(TEXT, bold=chosen))
        if editing is not None and chosen:
            if fresh:                          # whole value selected: typing replaces it
                put(scr, y, x0 + 22, editing[-(w - 25):], colors.attr(WARN, bold=True) | curses.A_REVERSE)
            else:
                put(scr, y, x0 + 22, (editing + "_")[-(w - 25):], colors.attr(WARN, bold=True))
        else:
            put(scr, y, x0 + 22, field_value(st, name), colors.attr(P1 if chosen else DIM, bold=chosen))
    help_text = HELP[FIELDS[sel]]
    if bad_input:
        centered(scr, y0 + h + 1, "that value is not valid", colors.attr(BAD, bold=True))
    elif help_text:
        for k, line in enumerate(wrap(help_text, min(cols - 4, w))):
            centered(scr, y0 + h + 1 + k, line, colors.attr(DIM))
    hint = ("type to replace" if fresh else "type") + ", Enter to save, Esc to cancel" if editing is not None else \
        "%s/%s select  %s  %s/%s or Enter change  %s  Q back" % (g.up, g.down, g.sep, g.left, g.right, g.sep)
    centered(scr, min(rows - 1, y0 + h + 4), hint, colors.attr(DIM))
    scr.refresh()


def wrap(text: str, width: int) -> List[str]:
    lines, cur = [], ""
    for word in text.split():
        if cur and len(cur) + 1 + len(word) > width:
            lines.append(cur)
            cur = word
        else:
            cur = (cur + " " + word).strip()
    if cur:
        lines.append(cur)
    return lines


def settings_screen(scr, colors: Colors, st: Settings) -> None:
    sel = 0
    editing: Optional[str] = None
    fresh = False                            # True until the first keystroke: value is "selected"
    bad = False
    while True:
        draw_settings(scr, colors, st, sel, editing, bad, fresh)
        ch = scr.getch()
        if ch == -1:
            continue
        if editing is not None:
            if ch in (10, 13, curses.KEY_ENTER):
                bad = not commit_edit(st, FIELDS[sel], editing)
                if not bad:
                    editing = None
                fresh = False
            elif ch == 27:
                editing, bad, fresh = None, False, False
            elif ch in (curses.KEY_BACKSPACE, 127, 8):
                editing, bad = ("" if fresh else editing[:-1]), False
                fresh = False
            elif 32 <= ch < 127:
                base = "" if fresh else editing
                if len(base) < 40:
                    editing, bad = base + chr(ch), False
                fresh = False
            continue
        bad = False
        name = FIELDS[sel]
        if ch in (curses.KEY_UP, ord("k"), ord("w"), ord("W")):
            sel = (sel - 1) % len(FIELDS)
        elif ch in (curses.KEY_DOWN, ord("j"), ord("s"), ord("S")):
            sel = (sel + 1) % len(FIELDS)
        elif ch in (curses.KEY_LEFT, ord("h"), ord("a"), ord("A")):
            cycle(st, name, -1)
        elif ch in (curses.KEY_RIGHT, ord("l"), ord("d"), ord("D")):
            cycle(st, name, 1)
        elif ch in (10, 13, curses.KEY_ENTER, ord(" ")):
            if name in ("host", "port"):
                editing, fresh = field_value(st, name), True
            elif name == "back":
                return
            else:
                cycle(st, name)
        elif ch in (ord("q"), ord("Q")):
            return


# =============================================================================
# menu loop and the three ways to start a game
# =============================================================================

def run_menu(scr, st: Settings, message: str) -> str:
    curses.curs_set(0)
    scr.keypad(True)
    scr.timeout(100)                         # wake up regularly so a resize redraws
    colors = Colors()
    colors.init()
    while True:
        draw_menu(scr, colors, st, message)
        ch = scr.getch()
        if ch == -1 or ch == curses.KEY_RESIZE:
            continue
        message = ""
        if ch in (curses.KEY_UP, ord("k"), ord("w"), ord("W")):
            st.selected = (st.selected - 1) % len(ITEMS)
        elif ch in (curses.KEY_DOWN, ord("j"), ord("s"), ord("S")):
            st.selected = (st.selected + 1) % len(ITEMS)
        elif ord("1") <= ch < ord("1") + len(ITEMS):
            st.selected = ch - ord("1")
            if ITEMS[st.selected][0] != "settings":
                return ITEMS[st.selected][0]
            settings_screen(scr, colors, st)
        elif ch in (10, 13, curses.KEY_ENTER, ord(" ")):
            key = ITEMS[st.selected][0]
            if key == "settings":
                settings_screen(scr, colors, st)
            else:
                return key
        elif ch in (ord("q"), ord("Q")):
            return "exit"


def play_local(st: Settings, watch: bool) -> str:
    """A private server on this machine plus bots.  Returns an error message, or ''."""
    server = GameServer("127.0.0.1", 0)
    clients: List[GameClient] = []
    try:
        server.start()
        human = None if watch else HumanInput(sticky=st.sticky)
        me = GameClient("127.0.0.1", server.port,
                        input_fn=human if human else Bot(error=st.bot_error))
        clients.append(me)
        me.join()                              # first in, so a human gets the left wall
        me.start()
        for _ in range(3):
            bot = GameClient("127.0.0.1", server.port, input_fn=Bot(error=st.bot_error))
            clients.append(bot)
            bot.join()
            bot.start()
        tui.run(me, human, 0, ascii_mode=st.ascii_mode)
        return ""
    except (JoinError, OSError) as exc:
        return "Could not start the local game: %s" % exc
    finally:
        for c in clients:
            c.leave()
        server.stop()


def play_remote(st: Settings) -> str:
    human = HumanInput(sticky=st.sticky)
    try:
        client = GameClient(st.host, st.port, input_fn=human)
    except OSError as exc:
        return "Cannot find server '%s': %s" % (st.host, exc)
    try:
        client.join()
    except JoinError as exc:
        client.leave()
        return "Could not join %s:%d - %s" % (st.host, st.port, exc)
    client.start()
    try:
        tui.run(client, human, 0, ascii_mode=st.ascii_mode)
    finally:
        client.leave()
    return ""


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python3 -m game", description="4-player pong: title screen and menu")
    ap.add_argument("--server", default="127.0.0.1", help="address 'Join a server' connects to")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=C.GAME_PORT)
    ap.add_argument("--ascii", action="store_true", help="plain ASCII graphics")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    exit_on_signals()
    try:
        locale.setlocale(locale.LC_ALL, "")
    except locale.Error:
        pass
    os.environ.setdefault("ESCDELAY", "25")

    st = Settings(host=args.server, port=args.port, graphics=2 if args.ascii else 0)
    message = ""
    try:
        while True:
            choice = curses.wrapper(run_menu, st, message)
            message = ""
            if choice == "exit":
                return 0
            if choice == "local":
                message = play_local(st, watch=False)
            elif choice == "watch":
                message = play_local(st, watch=True)
            elif choice == "join":
                message = play_remote(st)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: bot.py ===
"""A computer-controlled paddle.  Same interface as a human: snapshot in, -1/0/+1 out."""
from __future__ import annotations

import random
from typing import Optional

from .protocol import PHASE_PLAYING, Snapshot

DEADBAND = 0.02       # don't jitter when already lined up
REROLL_TICKS = 18     # re-pick the aiming error every 0.6 s at 30 Hz


class Bot:
    """Follows the ball along its wall, with a small random aiming error so bots
    sometimes miss (otherwise games would never end).  Pass `error=0` for a
    perfect bot."""

    def __init__(self, seed: Optional[int] = None, error: float = 0.14) -> None:
        self.rng = random.Random(seed)
        self.error = error
        self._bucket = -1
        self._offset = 0.0

    def __call__(self, snap: Optional[Snapshot], slot: int) -> int:
        if snap is None or snap.phase != PHASE_PLAYING:
            return 0
        if not snap.active[slot] or snap.lives[slot] <= 0:
            return 0
        bucket = snap.tick // REROLL_TICKS
        if bucket != self._bucket:
            self._bucket = bucket
            self._offset = self.rng.uniform(-self.error, self.error)
        ball = snap.ball_y if slot < 2 else snap.ball_x     # position along my wall
        diff = (ball + self._offset) - snap.paddles[slot]
        if abs(diff) < DEADBAND:
            return 0
        return 1 if diff > 0 else -1

=== FOLDER: /game ===
=== FILE: tui.py ===
"""Terminal UI (curses) and keyboard input for human players.

The arena is drawn with half-block characters, so every terminal cell holds two
square "pixels".  That makes the ball and paddles move smoothly, and the arena
stays square on screen whatever the terminal's cell shape.  Every wall is tinted
with its owner's colour (dim grey while nobody owns it); empty or eliminated
walls are solid, exactly like in the simulation.

Controls depend on which wall you own:
    left / right wall players (P1, P2):   W / Up = up      S / Down = down
    top / bottom wall players (P3, P4):   A / Left = left  D / Right = right
    Space = stop    Q = quit

Terminals only report key *presses* (never releases), so by default a key moves
your paddle for HOLD seconds; holding the key keeps it moving once your OS key
repeat starts.  In sticky mode a key sets a direction that lasts until Space or
the opposite key - far easier to play, so the menu uses it by default.
